===== .changeset/r8a-post-merge-attribution.md =====
---
"@olympus-ai/core": minor
"@olympus-ai/vault": minor
"@olympus-ai/api": minor
"@olympus-ai/conformance": minor
---

R8a: post-merge attribution and the baseline. A merged run now enters `observe` in the commit that spends its integrate grant, and leaves it once the policy's observation window has passed since GitHub's merge time, recorded in P14's record as `observation-closed`. Two templates feed it, and neither seats a model or starts a run. `observe-signal`, valid only on `post-merge` and `ci-failure`, records a push or a failed check against the change whose commit the event names, from the extracted fields alone, and records no candidate. `observe-history`, valid only on `scheduled`, walks the default branch's first-parent history: a later commit whose `git patch-id --stable` is a change's inverse is its rollback, and one that inverts some of its hunks is a partial reversal, reported apart and never counted as a rollback. Every first-parent change no run made is the baseline. Each signal is a write-once `ObservationRecord`, under its run or beside the runs, and each time says whether it is provider-reported, committer-claimed, or the runtime's. R2's report replaces the escaped false-done rate's `'not-established'` with a measured rate over runs whose window closed, and adds the change-failure rate per role and seat beside its baseline, rollback, partial-reversal, and check-failure counts, time to restore kept apart by time source, the baseline's lead time, and a statement that each bound assumes independent runs. `TriggerPolicy` gains `observe.windowMs`, required while an observe template is named; `TemplateId` gains both templates; `TriggerDecision` gains the `observed` outcome; `NightlyRecord` gains `observation`; the Vault gains `recordObservation` and `readObservations`; and the station machine gains the `observe` step.

===== packages/api/src/history.ts =====
/**
 * The default branch's history, read from a host-side checkout (R8a). It
 * lists first-parent commits, reads a change's diff, and decides whether a
 * later commit undoes one: a rollback when the later commit's patch id is the
 * change's inverse, a partial reversal when it inverts some of the change's
 * hunks and not all (D-A-OB-04, D-R8a-06). Nothing here reads a commit
 * message: a revert is found by what it does, never by what it says.
 *
 * Only plumbing commands run, each with `core.fsmonitor=false`, and every
 * diff with `--no-ext-diff` and `--no-textconv`, so no command the
 * repository's configuration names runs on a read.
 */
import { execFile, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';

const run = promisify(execFile);
const MAX_BUFFER = 256 * 1024 * 1024;
const OID = /^[0-9a-f]{40,64}$/u;

function gitArgs(checkout: string, ...args: string[]): string[] {
  return ['-C', checkout, '-c', 'core.fsmonitor=false', '-c', 'core.quotePath=false', ...args];
}

async function git(checkout: string, ...args: string[]): Promise<string> {
  const { stdout } = await run('git', gitArgs(checkout, ...args), { maxBuffer: MAX_BUFFER, encoding: 'utf8' });
  return stdout;
}

/** `git` with `input` on its standard input. */
function gitWith(checkout: string, input: string, ...args: string[]): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn('git', gitArgs(checkout, ...args), { stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
    let stdout = '';
    let stderr = '';
    child.stdout.setEncoding('utf8').on('data', (text: string) => { stdout += text; });
    child.stderr.setEncoding('utf8').on('data', (text: string) => { stderr += text; });
    child.on('error', reject);
    child.on('close', (code) => {
      if (code === 0) resolve(stdout);
      else reject(new Error(`git ${args[0] ?? ''} exited ${String(code)}: ${stderr.trim()}`));
    });
    child.stdin.end(input);
  });
}

function requireOid(value: string, what: string): string {
  if (!OID.test(value)) throw new Error(`history: ${what} '${value}' is not an object name`);
  return value;
}

/** One first-parent commit of the default branch, as git stores it. */
export interface BranchCommit {
  readonly commit: string;
  readonly parents: readonly string[];
  /** The committer date: committer-claimed (D-A-OB-06). */
  readonly committedAt: string;
}

/** Whether `ancestor` is `descendant` or one of its ancestors. */
export async function isAncestor(checkout: string, ancestor: string, descendant: string): Promise<boolean> {
  requireOid(ancestor, 'ancestor');
  requireOid(descendant, 'descendant');
  try {
    await run('git', gitArgs(checkout, 'merge-base', '--is-ancestor', ancestor, descendant));
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    // Exit 1 is "not an ancestor"; anything else is a read that failed, never an answer (I5).
    if (typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && 'code' in error && error.code === 1) return false;
    throw error;
  }
}

/** A commit's parents and committer date. */
export async function commitOf(checkout: string, commit: string): Promise<BranchCommit> {
  const out = (await git(checkout, 'rev-list', '--no-walk', '--parents', '--format=%cI', requireOid(commit, 'commit'))).trim().split('\n');
  return parseCommit(out[0] ?? '', out[1] ?? '');
}

function parseCommit(header: string, date: string): BranchCommit {
  // `rev-list --format` prints `commit <oid> <parents...>`, then the format line.
  const [word, commit = '', ...parents] = header.trim().split(' ');
  if (word !== 'commit') throw new Error(`history: unreadable rev-list header '${header}'`);
  const committedAt = new Date(date.trim()).toISOString();
  return { commit: requireOid(commit, 'commit'), parents: parents.map((p) => requireOid(p, 'parent')), committedAt };
}

/** First-parent commits after `from` up to and including `to`, oldest first; every first-parent commit of `to` where `from` is null. */
export async function firstParents(checkout: string, from: string | null, to: string): Promise<BranchCommit[]> {
  const range = from === null ? [requireOid(to, 'head')] : [requireOid(to, 'head'), `^${requireOid(from, 'start')}`];
  const lines = (await git(checkout, 'rev-list', '--first-parent', '--reverse', '--parents', '--format=%cI', ...range)).split('\n').filter((l) => l !== '');
  const out: BranchCommit[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < lines.length; i += 2) out.push(parseCommit(lines[i] ?? '', lines[i + 1] ?? ''));
  return out;
}

/** The empty tree's name in this repository's hash: a root commit's diff is against it. */
async function emptyTree(checkout: string): Promise<string> {
  return requireOid((await gitWith(checkout, '', 'mktree')).trim(), 'empty tree');
}

/** The patch from `a` to `b`, with no external diff or text conversion; `a` null is the empty tree. */
async function patch(checkout: string, a: string | null, b: string, context: 'default' | 'none'): Promise<string> {
  const left = a ?? (await emptyTree(checkout));
  return git(
    checkout, 'diff-tree', '-p', '-r', '--no-ext-diff', '--no-textconv', '--no-color', '--no-renames', '--full-index',
    ...(context === 'none' ? ['-U0'] : []), requireOid(left, 'tree'), requireOid(b, 'tree'),
  );
}

/** The `patch-id --stable` of a patch, or null for an empty one. */
async function patchId(checkout: string, text: string): Promise<string | null> {
  if (text.trim() === '') return null;
  const out = (await gitWith(checkout, text, 'patch-id', '--stable')).trim();
  const id = out.split(' ')[0] ?? '';
  return id === '' ? null : requireOid(id, 'patch id');
}

/**
 * One hunk at zero context: its file and the lines it removes and adds,
 * whitespace at each end trimmed as `patch-id` ignores it. Its inverse swaps
 * the two.
 */
function hunkKeys(text: string, inverse: boolean): string[] {
  const keys: string[] = [];
  let minus = '';
  let plus = '';
  let removed: string[] = [];
  let added: string[] = [];
  let inHunk = false;
  const flush = (): void => {
    if (inHunk && (removed.length > 0 || added.length > 0)) {
      const path = plus !== '/dev/null' ? plus : minus;
      const [from, to] = inverse ? [added, removed] : [removed, added];
      keys.push(JSON.stringify([path, from, to]));
    }
    removed = [];
    added = [];
  };
  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    if (line.startsWith('diff --git ')) {
      flush();
      inHunk = false;
    } else if (line.startsWith('--- ') && !inHunk) {
      minus = line.slice(4).replace(/^a\//u, '');
    } else if (line.startsWith('+++ ') && !inHunk) {
      plus = line.slice(4).replace(/^b\//u, '');
    } else if (line.startsWith('@@')) {
      flush();
      inHunk = true;
    } else if (inHunk && line.startsWith('-')) {
      removed.push(line.slice(1).trim());
    } else if (inHunk && line.startsWith('+')) {
      added.push(line.slice(1).trim());
    }
  }
  flush();
  return keys;
}

/** What `classifyReversal` compares a later commit against: a change's inverse, read once. */
export interface ChangeInverse {
  readonly change: string;
  /** Null for a change with an empty diff, which nothing can revert. */
  readonly inversePatchId: string | null;
  readonly inverseHunks: readonly string[];
}

/** A first-parent commit's own diff, as `classifyReversal` reads it. */
export interface CommitDiff {
  readonly commit: string;
  readonly patchId: string | null;
  readonly hunks: readonly string[];
}

/** A change's inverse: the diff from the change's merge commit back to its first parent. */
export async function changeInverse(checkout: string, change: BranchCommit): Promise<ChangeInverse> {
  const parent = change.parents[0] ?? null;
  const base = parent ?? (await emptyTree(checkout));
  return {
    change: change.commit,
    inversePatchId: await patchId(checkout, await patch(checkout, change.commit, base, 'default')),
    inverseHunks: hunkKeys(await patch(checkout, parent, change.commit, 'none'), true),
  };
}

/** A later commit's diff against its first parent. */
export async function commitDiff(checkout: string, commit: BranchCommit): Promise<CommitDiff> {
  const parent = commit.parents[0] ?? null;
  return {
    commit: commit.commit,
    patchId: await patchId(checkout, await patch(checkout, parent, commit.commit, 'default')),
    hunks: hunkKeys(await patch(checkout, parent, commit.commit, 'none'), false),
  };
}

export type Reversal =
  | { readonly kind: 'rollback' }
  | { readonly kind: 'partial-reversal'; readonly hunks: number; readonly of: number }
  | { readonly kind: 'none' };

/**
 * Whether `later` undoes `change`, by diff alone (D-A-OB-04). A rollback is
 * an exact inverse by patch id. A partial reversal inverts at least one and
 * not every one of the change's hunks; it is never a rollback.
 */
export function classifyReversal(change: ChangeInverse, later: CommitDiff): Reversal {
  if (change.inversePatchId === null || later.commit === change.change) return { kind: 'none' };
  if (later.patchId !== null && later.patchId === change.inversePatchId) return { kind: 'rollback' };
  const theirs = new Set(later.hunks);
  const hunks = change.inverseHunks.filter((h) => theirs.has(h)).length;
  return hunks > 0 && hunks < change.inverseHunks.length ? { kind: 'partial-reversal', hunks, of: change.inverseHunks.length } : { kind: 'none' };
}

/**
 * A change's lead time, from its first commit's author date to its merge
 * commit's committer date, both committer-claimed. A commit with one parent
 * keeps no first commit (D-A-BR-04).
 */
export async function leadTimeOf(checkout: string, change: BranchCommit): Promise<{ kind: 'measured'; ms: number; source: 'committer-claimed' } | { kind: 'not-measurable'; why: 'one-parent' }> {
  const [first, second] = change.parents;
  if (first === undefined || second === undefined) return { kind: 'not-measurable', why: 'one-parent' };
  const dates = (await git(checkout, 'rev-list', '--reverse', '--format=%aI', '--no-commit-header', requireOid(second, 'parent'), `^${requireOid(first, 'parent')}`))
    .split('\n').filter((l) => l !== '');
  const earliest = dates[0];
  if (earliest === undefined) return { kind: 'not-measurable', why: 'one-parent' };
  return { kind: 'measured', ms: Date.parse(change.committedAt) - Date.parse(earliest), source: 'committer-claimed' };
}

 /**
  * How a trigger event becomes an intake candidate (R22). A delivery arrives
  * on `POST /events/<subscription>`, the one route outside the local token, and
  * is refused at the first check it fails:
  *
  * 1. the subscription is one the policy names, before the body is read (I4);
  * 2. the body is within its byte cap, held to it while it is read;
  * 3. its HMAC-SHA256 under the subscription's secret verifies, in constant
  *    time, before anything parses it;
  * 4. its digest is not one the Vault holds, so a body sent again is refused
  *    before it is parsed (D-R22-02);
  * 5. its own signed time is within the policy's age, and its fields are the
  *    kind's schema, checked whole (I7);
  * 6. the budget has room.
  *
  * The first three, and a replay, are counted per subscription and hour and
  * never written one by one: anyone can send them (D-R22-03). Every other
  * outcome is written once, under the body's digest, with its candidate in the
  * same write. A candidate starts nothing: a person reads it and may admit a
  * run from it through `POST /runs` (D-A-TF-01).
  *
  * The secret is resolved on the host, from a variable the subscription names
  * and only one with the webhook prefix (D-A-TF-05). No sandbox spec carries
  * it, and no driver request carries any byte of a body.
  */
 import {
-  WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREFIX, checkFields, isJobTemplate, type AuthorTrust, type CandidateId, type JobSchema, type JobTemplateId, type Policy, type RunId,
+  WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREFIX, checkFields, isJobTemplate, isSignalTemplate, type AuthorTrust, type CandidateId, type JobSchema, type JobTemplateId, type Policy, type RunId,
   type TemplateId, type TriggerLineage, type TriggerSchedule, type TriggerSubscription,
 } from '@olympus-ai/core';

     this.#secrets = options.secrets;

   /** The subscription a delivery names, or null; null is counted as `not-subscribed` and the body is never read. */
   subscription(policy: Policy, id: string): TriggerSubscription | null {
     return (policy.triggers.subscriptions ?? []).find((s) => s.id === id) ?? null;

   /** Counts a refusal that writes no record of its own (D-R22-03). */
   async count(bucket: string, cause: CountedRefusalCause): Promise<DeliveryOutcome> {
     await this.#vault.countRefusedDelivery(bucket, utcHour(this.#now()), cause);

   /** Steps 3 to 6, for a body already read within its cap. */
   async deliver(policy: Policy, subscription: TriggerSubscription, signature: string | undefined, body: Uint8Array): Promise<DeliveryOutcome> {
     return this.#serial(async () => {
       const name = subscription.secret;
       const secret = name.startsWith(WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREFIX) ? (this.#secrets(name) ?? '') : '';
       if (!signatureVerifies(body, signature, secret)) return this.count(subscription.id, 'unverified');
       const digest = bodyDigest(body);
       if (await this.#vault.hasTriggerDecision(subscription.id, digest)) return this.count(subscription.id, 'replayed');
       const now = this.#now();
       const decide = (outcome: TriggerDecision['outcome']): TriggerDecision => ({
         source: subscription.id, key: digest, decidedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(), outcome, collectedBy: 'runtime',
       });
       const write = async (decision: TriggerDecision, candidate: CandidateRecord | null, result: DeliveryOutcome): Promise<DeliveryOutcome> => {
         // A key written between the check and here is the same body sent twice: the second is a replay.
         if (!(await this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(decision, candidate))) return this.count(subscription.id, 'replayed');
         return result;
       };
       if (!policy.triggers.enabled.includes(subscription.kind)) {
         return write(decide({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' });

       const extracted = extractGithub(subscription, untrustedBody(body, `github:${subscription.id}`), now, maxAge);
       if (!extracted.ok) {
         if (extracted.outcome === 'ignored') return write(decide({ kind: 'ignored' }), null, { outcome: 'ignored' });

           return write(decide({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'stale', signedAt: new Date(extracted.signedAt).toISOString() }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'stale' });
         }
         return write(decide({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'schema', defects: extracted.defects }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'schema' });
       }

+      const routed = policy.triggers.taskTemplate[extracted.kind];
+      if (isSignalTemplate(routed)) {
+        if (this.#jobs === null || (extracted.kind !== 'post-merge' && extracted.kind !== 'ci-failure')) {
+          return write(decide({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' });
+        }
+        // The extracted fields alone, checked whole against the kind's schema; nothing else in the body is read (I7).
+        const observation = await this.#jobs.signal(extracted.kind, extracted.extracted, extracted.signedAt);
+        return write(decide({ kind: 'observed', observation }), null, { outcome: 'observed' });
+      }
       if (await this.#overBudget(policy, now)) return write(decide({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'budget' }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'budget' });
       const template = policy.triggers.taskTemplate[extracted.kind];
       if (template === undefined) return write(decide({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' });

   }
 
   /**
    * A schedule's firing for a UTC date: a candidate whose one field is the
    * schedule's id, written once under `schedule:<id>` and the date. Its
    * author is the policy's, so its trust is `owner`.
    */
   async fire(policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string): Promise<DeliveryOutcome | null> {
     const template = schedule.template;
     if (isJobTemplate(template)) return this.#fireJob(policy, { ...schedule, template }, date);
     return this.#serial(async () => {
       const source = `schedule:${schedule.id}`;
       const now = this.#now();
       const decision = (outcome: TriggerDecision['outcome']): TriggerDecision => ({ source, key: date, decidedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(), outcome, collectedBy: 'runtime' });
       const write = async (d: TriggerDecision, c: CandidateRecord | null, result: DeliveryOutcome): Promise<DeliveryOutcome | null> => ((await this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(d, c)) ? result : null);
       if (!policy.triggers.enabled.includes('scheduled')) return write(decision({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' });
       if (await this.#overBudget(policy, now)) return write(decision({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'budget' }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'budget' });
       const fields = checkFields('scheduled', { schedule: schedule.id });
       if (!fields.ok) return write(decision({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'schema', defects: fields.defects }), null, { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'schema' });
       const id = intakeId(source, date);
       const candidate: CandidateRecord = {
         id, kind: 'scheduled', template: schedule.template satisfies TemplateId, schema: 'scheduled', extracted: fields.fields, authorTrust: 'owner' satisfies AuthorTrust,
         lineage: { depth: 0, chain: [], windowStart: new Date(now).toISOString() }, signedAt: null, body: null,
         recordedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(), collectedBy: 'runtime',
       };
       return write(decision({ kind: 'candidate', candidate: id }), candidate, { outcome: 'candidate', candidate: id });
     });
   }
 
   /**
    * A job's firing (R13a). The job runs outside the turn, so a long scan holds
    * no delivery up. The firing is written under `schedule:<id>` and the date,
    * naming the record the job wrote, so the date is covered and a missed one
    * is still found (D-R13a-07). A date already written runs nothing.
    */
   async #fireJob(policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule & { readonly template: JobTemplateId }, date: string): Promise<DeliveryOutcome | null> {
     const source = `schedule:${schedule.id}`;
     const decision = (outcome: TriggerDecision['outcome']): TriggerDecision => ({ source, key: date, decidedAt: new Date(this.#now()).toISOString(), outcome, collectedBy: 'runtime' });
     if (await this.#vault.hasTriggerDecision(source, date)) return null;
     if (!policy.triggers.enabled.includes('scheduled') || this.#jobs === null) {
       const refused = await this.#serial(() => this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(decision({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' }), null));
       return refused ? { outcome: 'refused', cause: 'not-enabled' } : null;
     }
     const { sha256 } = await this.#jobs.run(policy, schedule, date, this);
     const written = await this.#serial(() => this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(decision({ kind: 'job', nightly: sha256 }), null));
     return written ? { outcome: 'ignored' } : null;
   }
 
   /**
    * A nightly finding as a candidate (A-R13a-03, A-R13a-07, A-R13b-01). Its
    * kind is `scheduled`, so it is admitted under the schedule's route; its
    * fields are checked whole against its job schema, a scanner's
    * `nightly-finding` or a reviewer's; and it is held to the same budget
    * as every candidate. It is written under `nightly:<schedule id>`, apart
    * from the firing's own source, whose keys are dates.
    */
   async recordFinding(
     policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule, key: string, fields: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, schema: JobSchema = 'nightly-finding',
   ): Promise<{ readonly id: CandidateId } | { readonly none: 'budget' | 'schema' | 'recorded-before' }> {
     return this.#serial(async () => {
       const source = `nightly:${schedule.id}`;
       const now = this.#now();
       const decision = (outcome: TriggerDecision['outcome']): TriggerDecision => ({ source, key, decidedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(), outcome, collectedBy: 'runtime' });
       if (await this.#vault.hasTriggerDecision(source, key)) return { none: 'recorded-before' };
       const checked = checkFields(schema, fields);
       if (!checked.ok) {
         await this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(decision({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'schema', defects: checked.defects }), null);
         return { none: 'schema' };
       }
       if (await this.#overBudget(policy, now)) {
         await this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(decision({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'budget' }), null);
         return { none: 'budget' };
       }
       const id = intakeId(source, key);
       const extracted: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = checked.fields;
       const candidate: CandidateRecord = {
         id, kind: 'scheduled', template: schedule.template, schema, extracted, authorTrust: 'owner',
         lineage: { depth: 0, chain: [], windowStart: new Date(now).toISOString() }, signedAt: null, body: null,
         recordedAt: new Date(now).toISOString(), collectedBy: 'runtime',
       };
       return (await this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision(decision({ kind: 'candidate', candidate: id }), candidate)) ? { id } : { none: 'recorded-before' };
     });
   }
 
   /** Records firings a stopped service missed, as one entry, and never runs them (D-A-TF-08). */
   async missed(schedule: TriggerSchedule, first: string, last: string): Promise<void> {
     await this.#serial(async () => {
       await this.#vault.recordTriggerDecision({
         source: `schedule:${schedule.id}`, key: `missed:${first}:${last}`, decidedAt: new Date(this.#now()).toISOString(),
         outcome: { kind: 'missed', first, last }, collectedBy: 'runtime',
       }, null);
     });
   }
 
   async #serial<T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
     const run = this.#turn.then(work, work);
     this.#turn = run.catch(() => undefined);
     return run;
   }
 
   /** Candidates recorded across every kind in the sliding window, against the policy's budget (D-R22-07). */
   async #overBudget(policy: Policy, now: number): Promise<boolean> {
     const { runs, windowMs } = policy.triggers.budgetPerWindow;
     const since = now - windowMs;
     let recorded = 0;
     for (const entry of await this.#vault.readTriggerDecisions()) {
       if (entry.candidate !== null && Date.parse(entry.candidate.recordedAt) > since) recorded += 1;
     }
     return recorded >= runs;
   }
 
   /**
    * An event naming a commit an Olympus run merged carries that run's chain
    * plus the run, one deeper. Read from the runtime's own integration and
    * admission records, never from the payload's say-so.
    */
   async #lineage(commit: string | null, now: number): Promise<TriggerLineage> {
     const windowStart = new Date(now).toISOString();
     if (commit === null) return { depth: 0, chain: [], windowStart };
     for (const runId of await this.#vault.listRuns()) {
       const merged = (await readIntegration(this.#vault, runId)).some((r) => r.kind === 'merged' && r.mergeCommit === commit);
       if (!merged) continue;
       const parent = await this.#parentLineage(runId);
       return { depth: parent.depth + 1, chain: [...parent.chain, runId], windowStart };
     }
     return { depth: 0, chain: [], windowStart };
   }
 
   async #parentLineage(runId: RunId): Promise<TriggerLineage> {
     const state = await this.#vault.readRunState(runId);
     const record = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(await this.#vault.read(state.admission))) as AdmissionRecord;
     return record.run.trigger.lineage;
   }
 }

 /**
  * One decision's typed fields. Every cause is listed, so a cause added later
  * fails to compile here rather than reaching the seat unprojected. No
  * message, detail, host, path, check id, or task id is taken: a check id may
  * be one a drafter wrote (R19), a host is the agent's choice, and a task id is
  * the requester's text, admitted with no character class (I7).
  */
 function decisionFields(d: EnforcementDecision, violation: IntegrityViolation | null): Record<string, string | number | null> {
   const base = { cause: d.decision.cause, decidedBy: d.decision.decidedBy, station: d.station };
   const decision = d.decision;
   switch (decision.cause) {
     case 'record-drift': return { ...base, problem: decision.problem };
+    case 'observation-closed': return { ...base, windowMs: decision.windowMs };
     case 'admission-refused': return { ...base, reason: decision.refusal.reason };
     case 'resume-refused': return { ...base, reason: decision.refusal.reason };
     case 'station-refused': {
       const refusal = decision.refusal;
       if (refusal.reason === 'gate-failed') {
         return { ...base, reason: refusal.reason, failedChecks: refusal.failed.length, failedCauses: [...new Set(refusal.failed.map((f) => f.cause))].sort().join(',') };
       }
       return { ...base, reason: refusal.reason };
     }
     case 'approval-granted': return { ...base, key: decision.key };
     case 'approval-refused': return { ...base, reason: decision.reason };
     case 'approval-rejected': return { ...base, reason: decision.reason, notes: decision.notes ? 'yes' : 'no' };
     case 'draft-sent-back': return { ...base, sentBackBy: decision.sentBack.by };
     case 'draft-floor-applied': return { ...base, level: decision.level, failedConditions: [...decision.failed].sort().join(',') };
     case 'egress-connection': return { ...base, verdict: decision.connection.verdict };
     case 'violation-recorded': return { ...base, violation: violation?.kind ?? null, role: violation?.role ?? null };
     case 'relay-refused': return { ...base, refused: decision.refused, exhausted: decision.exhausted };
     case 'tier-escalated': return { ...base, from: decision.from, to: decision.to, failedGates: decision.failedGates };
     case 'task-conflict': return { ...base, with: decision.with.length, paths: decision.paths.length };
   }
 }

   async run(policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule & { readonly template: JobTemplateId }, date: string, intake: Intake): Promise<{ record: NightlyRecord; sha256: string }> {

   async #integrity(schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string): Promise<Part> {
     const { vault } = this.#options;
     const findings: NightlyFinding[] = [];
     const refusals: NightlyRefusal[] = [];
     let prior: NightlyEntry | undefined;
     try {
       prior = [...(await vault.readNightly())]
         .filter((e) => e.record.schedule === schedule.id && e.record.integrity !== null && e.record.date < date)
         .at(-1);
     } catch (error) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `the nightly records could not be read: ${describe(error)}` });
     }
     // Each earlier firing names the record its job wrote. A baseline no firing names was not written by this job (codex-4, gemini-2).
     const named = new Map<string, string>();
     for (const entry of await vault.readTriggerDecisions()) {
       const { source, key, outcome } = entry.decision;
       if (source === `schedule:${schedule.id}` && outcome.kind === 'job') named.set(key, outcome.nightly);
     }
     if (prior !== undefined && named.get(prior.record.date) !== prior.sha256) {
       findings.push(drift(null, `nightly/${prior.sha256}.json`, 'changed-since-snapshot', named.get(prior.record.date) ?? 'unnamed', prior.sha256));
       refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'baseline-unrecorded', detail: `the record for ${prior.record.date} is not the one that night's firing names, so nothing is compared against it` });
       prior = undefined;
     }

     const hashes: Record<string, string> = {};
     let objects = 0;
     let storeRead = true;
     try {
       for await (const object of vault.listObjects()) {
         objects += 1;
         const observed = sha256(object.bytes);
         hashes[object.path] = observed;
         if (object.named === 'content') {

           if (observed !== expected) findings.push(drift(object.runId, object.path, 'hash-mismatch', expected, observed));
         }
       }
     } catch (error) {
       storeRead = false;
       refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });

     // Every record a firing names is still held.
     if (storeRead) {
       for (const [day, hash] of named) {
         if (hashes[`nightly/${hash}.json`] === undefined) findings.push(drift(null, `nightly/${hash}.json`, 'missing', hash, `missing (named by the firing of ${day})`));
       }
     }
 
     // Against the last night: a record that changed or went missing since it was hashed.
     const priorCoverage = prior?.record.integrity ?? null;
     if (storeRead && prior !== undefined && priorCoverage !== null) {
       for (const [path, expected] of Object.entries(priorCoverage.hashes)) {
         if (stillOpen(path, prior.record.startedAt)) continue;
         const observed = hashes[path];
         const runId = /^runs\/([^/]+)\//u.exec(path)?.[1] as RunId | undefined;
         if (observed === undefined) findings.push(drift(runId ?? null, path, 'missing', expected, 'missing'));
         else if (observed !== expected) findings.push(drift(runId ?? null, path, 'changed-since-snapshot', expected, observed));
       }
     }
 
     // Refs, locks, and rates, run by run.
     const records = new Map<RunId, RunRecords>();
     let ratesRead = true;
     for (const runId of await vault.listRuns()) {
       let state: RunState | null = null;
       try {
         state = await vault.readRunState(runId);
       } catch (error) {
         if (!isNoRunState(error)) {
           refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}` });
           continue;
         }
       }
       if (storeRead && state !== null) {
         for (const ref of refsOf(state)) {

           const observed = hashes[path] ?? (ref.kind === 'base-run' ? hashes[`runs/${runId}/base-run.json`] : undefined);

         }

       } catch (error) {

     }

     let rates: RateSnapshot | null = null;
     if (ratesRead) {

         const again: RunRecords[] = [];
         for (const runId of priorRates.runs) {
           const run = records.get(runId);
           if (run === undefined) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate: `runs.${runId}`, snapshot: '"settled"', observed: 'absent' });
           else {
-            if (!SETTLED.has(run.ending)) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate: `runs.${runId}`, snapshot: '"settled"', observed: JSON.stringify(run.ending) });
+            if (!settledForRates(run)) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate: `runs.${runId}`, snapshot: '"settled"', observed: JSON.stringify(run.ending) });
             again.push(run);
           }
         }
         const figures = flattenFigures(deriveOutcomes(again));
         for (const rate of new Set([...Object.keys(priorRates.figures), ...Object.keys(figures)])) {
           const before = priorRates.figures[rate] ?? 'absent';
           const now = figures[rate] ?? 'absent';
           if (before !== now) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate, snapshot: before, observed: now });
         }
       }
-      const settled = [...records.values()].filter((r) => SETTLED.has(r.ending));
+      const settled = [...records.values()].filter(settledForRates);
       rates = { runs: settled.map((r) => r.runId).sort(), figures: flattenFigures(deriveOutcomes(settled)) };
     }
 
     // Drift that names a run is also in that run's P14 record (A-R13a-04).
     for (const finding of findings) {
       if (finding.kind !== 'record-drift' || finding.runId === null) continue;
       await vault.recordDecision({
         runId: finding.runId, taskId: null, station: null, decidedAt: new Date(this.#now()).toISOString(), occurrence: randomUUID(),
         decision: { cause: 'record-drift', decidedBy: 'nightly', record: finding.record, problem: finding.problem, expected: finding.expected, observed: finding.observed },
         collectedBy: 'runtime',
       });
     }
 
     const integrity: IntegrityCoverage = {
       objects, hashes, comparedWith: prior === undefined ? 'none' : { record: prior.sha256 }, rates,
     };
     return { findings, refusals, integrity, security: null };
   }
 
   async #security(policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string, intake: Intake): Promise<Part> {
     const { target } = this.#options;
     const findings: NightlyFinding[] = [];
     const refusals: NightlyRefusal[] = [];
     const nightly = policy.triggers.nightly;
     const egress: Array<SecurityCoverage['egress'][number]> = [];
     const coverage = (commit: { branch: string; commit: string } | null, ran: SecurityCoverage['ran'], audited: SecurityCoverage['audited']): SecurityCoverage => ({ target: commit, ran, audited, egress });
     if (nightly === undefined) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'policy', cause: 'no-policy', detail: 'the policy names no nightly block, so no scanner or advisory host is granted' });
       return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(null, [], null) };
     }
     if (target === null) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'target', cause: 'no-target', detail: 'this host names no default branch to scan' });
       return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(null, [], null) };
     }
     const dir = join(this.#options.workspaces.root, 'nightly', `${schedule.id}-${date}-${randomUUID()}`);
     const tree = join(dir, 'tree');
     const ran: Array<SecurityCoverage['ran'][number]> = [];
     let commit: string;
     let files: readonly string[];
     try {
       commit = await resolveCommit(target.checkout, `refs/heads/${target.branch}`);
       await mkdir(tree, { recursive: true });
       files = (await materialize(target.checkout, commit, tree)).files;
     } catch (error) {
       await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
       refusals.push({ part: 'target', cause: 'target-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
       return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(null, [], null) };
     }
     const where = { branch: target.branch, commit };
     let audited: SecurityCoverage['audited'] = null;
     try {
       for (const [index, selected] of nightly.scanners.entries()) {
         const outcome = await this.#scan(tree, join(dir, `scan-${String(index)}`), selected);
         if (outcome.egress !== null) egress.push({ part: selected.kind, log: outcome.egress });
         if (!outcome.ok) {
           refusals.push({ part: selected.kind, cause: 'scan-failed', detail: outcome.reason });
           continue;
         }
         ran.push(selected.kind);
         for (const f of outcome.findings) {
           findings.push(await this.#finding(intake, policy, schedule, date, selected.kind, f.rule, f.location.path, f.location.line, f.fingerprint));
         }
       }
       const audit = await this.#audit(tree, files, join(dir, 'audit'), nightly.advisoryHost);
       if (audit.egress !== null) egress.push({ part: 'dependency-audit', log: audit.egress });
       if (!audit.ok) refusals.push({ part: 'dependency-audit', cause: audit.cause, detail: audit.detail });
       else {
         ran.push('dependency-audit');
         audited = { lockfile: audit.auditor.lockfile, stack: audit.auditor.stack };
         for (const f of audit.findings) {
           findings.push(await this.#finding(intake, policy, schedule, date, 'dependency-audit', f.rule, audit.auditor.lockfile, f.line, f.fingerprint));
         }
       }
     } finally {
       await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
     }
     return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(where, ran, audited) };
   }
 
   /** One R17 scanner over the tree, as `verify` runs it: a copy, `deny-all`, the pinned image, the tree the only data mount. */
   async #scan(
     tree: string, scratch: string, selected: NightlyScanner,
   ): Promise<({ ok: true; findings: readonly Finding[] } | { ok: false; reason: string }) & { egress: EgressLog | null }> {
     const scanner = scannerFor(selected.kind, this.#options.scanners);
     const check: SecurityCheckSpec = { id: `nightly-${selected.kind}`, kind: selected.kind, required: true, timeoutMs: NIGHTLY_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS, image: scanner.image, config: selected.config };
     const scanned = await scanTree({
       sandbox: this.#options.sandbox, tree, scratch: { dir: scratch, tree: join(scratch, 'tree'), config: join(scratch, 'config') },
       check, scanner, limits: this.#options.limits, user: this.#options.workspaces.user,
     });
     if (!scanned.ok) return { ok: false, reason: scanned.reason, egress: scanned.at === 'exec' ? scanned.egress : null };
     if (scanned.result.findings === null) return { ok: false, reason: `the scanner exited ${String(scanned.result.exitCode)} without a whole report`, egress: scanned.egress };
     return { ok: true, findings: scanned.result.findings, egress: scanned.egress };
   }
 
   /** The lockfile's audit in its own sandbox: the manifest and lockfile alone, read-only, egress to the advisory host alone (D-R13a-06). */
   async #audit(
     tree: string, files: readonly string[], scratch: string, advisoryHost: string,
   ): Promise<({ ok: true; auditor: DependencyAuditor; findings: readonly AuditFinding[] } | { ok: false; cause: NightlyRefusal['cause']; detail: string }) & { egress: EgressLog | null }> {
     const root = new Set(files.filter((f) => !f.includes('/')));
     const auditors = this.#options.auditors ?? AUDITORS;
     const lockfiles = [...auditors.map((a) => a.lockfile), ...UNSUPPORTED_LOCKFILES].filter((name) => root.has(name));
     // Lockfiles of two stacks: auditing whichever is listed first would leave the other unaudited (gemini-3).
     if (lockfiles.length > 1) return { ok: false, cause: 'unsupported-stack', detail: `the default branch holds ${lockfiles.join(' and ')}; which stack it uses is not decided`, egress: null };
     const auditor = auditors.find((a) => root.has(a.lockfile));
     if (auditor === undefined) {
       const other = UNSUPPORTED_LOCKFILES.find((name) => root.has(name));
       return other === undefined
         ? { ok: false, cause: 'no-lockfile', detail: 'the default branch holds no lockfile at its root', egress: null }
         : { ok: false, cause: 'unsupported-stack', detail: `${other} is a lockfile no audit covers`, egress: null };
     }
     await mkdir(scratch, { recursive: true });
     for (const name of auditor.files) if (root.has(name)) await copyFile(join(tree, name), join(scratch, name));
     const lockfileText = await readFile(join(scratch, auditor.lockfile), 'utf8');
     const spec: SandboxSpec = {
       image: auditor.image,
       mounts: { workspace: { source: scratch, target: '/workspace', mode: 'ro' }, others: [] },
       egress: { mode: 'allowlist', allow: [advisoryHost] },
       limits: this.#options.limits === null ? { cpus: 0, memoryMb: 0, pids: 0, wallClockMs: NIGHTLY_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS } : { ...this.#options.limits, wallClockMs: NIGHTLY_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS },
       user: { ...this.#options.workspaces.user },
     };
     let handle: SandboxHandle;
     try {
       handle = await this.#options.sandbox.provision(spec);
     } catch (error) {
       return { ok: false, cause: 'audit-failed', detail: `its sandbox could not be provisioned: ${describe(error)}`, egress: null };
     }
     let outcome: { ok: true; auditor: DependencyAuditor; findings: readonly AuditFinding[] } | { ok: false; cause: NightlyRefusal['cause']; detail: string };
     let egress: EgressLog;
     try {
       const exec = await this.#options.sandbox.exec(handle, [...auditor.invocation], { env: { HOME: '/tmp', COREPACK_ENABLE_DOWNLOAD_PROMPT: '0' } });
       const parsed = auditor.parse(exec.stdout, exec.exitCode, lockfileText);
       if (parsed.ok) outcome = { ok: true, auditor, findings: parsed.findings };
       else {
         outcome = parsed.unreachable
           ? { ok: false, cause: 'advisory-unreachable', detail: `the audit could not reach ${advisoryHost} (exit ${String(exec.exitCode)})` }
           : { ok: false, cause: 'audit-failed', detail: `the audit exited ${String(exec.exitCode)} without a whole report` };
       }
     } catch (error) {
       outcome = { ok: false, cause: 'audit-failed', detail: describe(error) };
     } finally {
       // Read after the audit whatever it did, so the record holds what the proxy decided (D-P14-04).
       ({ egress } = await this.#options.sandbox.destroy(handle));
     }
     // A report counts only where the proxy saw the advisory host reached; a grant is permission, not contact (codex-2).
     const reached = egress.kind === 'proxied' && egress.connections.some((c) => c.verdict !== 'refused' && c.host === advisoryHost);
     if (outcome.ok && !reached) {
       return { ok: false, cause: 'advisory-unreachable', detail: `the audit's proxy recorded no connection to ${advisoryHost}`, egress };
     }
     return { ...outcome, egress };
   }
 
   /** A finding, with the candidate it became or why it became none (D-R13a-07). */
   async #finding(
     intake: Intake, policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string,
     scanner: SecurityCoverage['ran'][number], rule: string, path: string, line: number | null, fingerprint: string,
   ): Promise<NightlyFinding> {
     // Checked before any record holds them: an unlocated finding is checked with a stand-in line, which is not kept.
     const checked = checkFields('nightly-finding', { scanner, rule, path, line: String(line ?? 1) });
     if (!checked.ok) return { kind: 'security-unrecordable', scanner, defects: checked.defects };
     if (!FINGERPRINT.test(fingerprint)) return { kind: 'security-unrecordable', scanner, defects: [{ field: 'fingerprint', problem: 'out-of-class' }] };
     const candidate = line === null
       ? { none: 'unlocated' as const }
       : await intake.recordFinding(policy, schedule, `${date}:${scanner}:${fingerprint}`, { scanner, rule, path, line: String(line) });
     return { kind: 'security', scanner, rule, path, line, fingerprint, candidate };
   }
 }

===== packages/api/src/observe.ts =====
/**
 * Post-merge observation (R8a, `docs/plan/R8-observe.md`). Two templates
 * feed it, and neither seats a model, opens a change, or starts a run
 * (D-A-LC-03):
 *
 * - `observe-signal` records a `post-merge` push or a `ci-failure` against
 *   the change whose commit the event names, read from the kind's extracted
 *   fields alone and attributed by commit identity alone (I7, D-A-OB-02). A
 *   commit no run merged is kept beside the runs, unattributed.
 * - `observe-history` walks the default branch's first-parent history from
 *   the last head it recorded, finds rollbacks and partial reversals by diff
 *   alone (D-A-OB-04), opens a window for every change no run made, and
 *   closes every window whose time has passed (D-A-OB-03). A history it
 *   cannot read is a refusal, never an empty walk (I5).
 *
 * Every record is the runtime's: from git, from a signed body's typed
 * fields, or from the runtime's clock, and each time says which (D-A-OB-06).
 */
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Policy, RunId } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import { resolveCommit } from '@olympus-ai/readiness';
import {
  isNoRunState, type NightlyRefusal, type ObservationCoverage, type ObservationEntry, type ObservationRecord, type ObservationSignal,
  type OpenedChange, type SourcedTime, type Vault,
} from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import {
  changeInverse, classifyReversal, commitDiff, commitOf, firstParents, isAncestor, leadTimeOf,
  type BranchCommit, type ChangeInverse, type CommitDiff,
} from './history.js';
import { readIntegration } from './line.js';

/** The checkout the walk reads and the branch runs merge into: the target's default branch. */
export interface ObserveTarget {
  readonly checkout: string;
  readonly branch: string;
}

/** A run's merge, as its integration record holds it. */
interface MergedRun {
  readonly runId: RunId;
  readonly mergeCommit: string;
  readonly mergedAt: string;
}

function describe(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof Error) return error.message;
  return typeof error === 'string' ? error : 'unknown error';
}

/** Every run's merge, by merge commit, from the runtime's own integration records: never a payload's say-so. */
async function mergedRuns(vault: Vault): Promise<Map<string, MergedRun>> {
  const out = new Map<string, MergedRun>();
  for (const runId of await vault.listRuns()) {
    for (const r of await readIntegration(vault, runId)) {
      if (r.kind === 'merged') out.set(r.mergeCommit, { runId, mergeCommit: r.mergeCommit, mergedAt: r.mergedAt });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * One signal from a verified delivery's extracted fields (D-A-OB-02). The
 * change is the commit the event names; the run is the one whose recorded
 * merge commit that is, or none. `signedAt` is the body's own time, which
 * GitHub signed: provider-reported.
 */
export async function recordSignal(
  vault: Vault, now: () => number, kind: 'post-merge' | 'ci-failure', fields: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, signedAt: number,
): Promise<string> {
  const commit = fields.commit;
  if (commit === undefined) throw new Error(`observe: a ${kind} signal's fields name no commit`);
  const at: SourcedTime = { at: new Date(signedAt).toISOString(), source: 'provider-reported' };
  let signal: ObservationSignal;
  if (kind === 'ci-failure') {
    const { checkRun, conclusion } = fields;
    if (checkRun === undefined || conclusion === undefined) throw new Error('observe: a ci-failure signal names no check run or conclusion');
    signal = { kind: 'check-failure', commit, at, checkRun, conclusion };
  } else {
    signal = { kind: 'pushed', commit, at };
  }
  const run = (await mergedRuns(vault)).get(commit);
  const record: ObservationRecord = {
    runId: run?.runId ?? null, change: commit, signal, recordedAt: new Date(now()).toISOString(), collectedBy: 'runtime',
  };
  return vault.recordObservation(record);
}

export interface Walked {
  readonly refusals: NightlyRefusal[];
  readonly observation: ObservationCoverage;
}

/** One change whose window is open, with what the walk compares later commits against. */
interface OpenChange {
  readonly change: string;
  readonly runId: RunId | null;
  readonly opened: SourcedTime;
  inverse?: ChangeInverse;
}

const closes = (entries: readonly ObservationEntry[]): boolean => entries.some((e) => e.record.signal.kind === 'window-closed');

/**
 * The walk (D-A-OB-02, D-R8a-06 to D-R8a-10). Reversals are recorded before
 * any window is closed, so a revert the walk reaches counts against a window
 * that closes in the same walk (D-R8a-07).
 */
export async function walkHistory(vault: Vault, target: ObserveTarget | null, policy: Policy, now: () => number): Promise<Walked> {
  const refusals: NightlyRefusal[] = [];
  const coverage = { branch: target?.branch ?? null, from: null as string | null, to: null as string | null, walked: 0, opened: [] as OpenedChange[], closed: [] as string[], signals: 0 };
  const done = (): Walked => ({ refusals, observation: { ...coverage } });
  const windowMs = policy.triggers.observe?.windowMs;
  if (windowMs === undefined) {
    refusals.push({ part: 'policy', cause: 'no-window', detail: 'the policy names no observation window, so no window can close' });
    return done();
  }
  if (target === null) {
    refusals.push({ part: 'target', cause: 'no-target', detail: 'this host names no default branch to walk' });
    return done();
  }
  const { checkout, branch } = target;
  const stamp = (): string => new Date(now()).toISOString();

  // What the store already holds: every merge, every observation, and every earlier walk.
  const merged = await mergedRuns(vault);
  const runObservations = new Map<RunId, readonly ObservationEntry[]>();
  for (const run of merged.values()) runObservations.set(run.runId, await vault.readObservations(run.runId));
  const beside = await vault.readObservations(null);
  const closedBaseline = new Set(beside.filter((e) => e.record.signal.kind === 'window-closed').map((e) => e.record.change));
  const walks = (await vault.readNightly()).filter((e) => e.record.observation !== null);
  const lastTo = [...walks].reverse().find((e) => e.record.observation?.to != null)?.record.observation?.to ?? null;
  const openedBefore = new Map<string, OpenedChange>();
  for (const w of walks) for (const o of w.record.observation?.opened ?? []) if (!closedBaseline.has(o.change)) openedBefore.set(o.change, o);
  // A provider-reported landing time, where a push was recorded for a commit (D-R8a-08).
  const pushed = new Map<string, SourcedTime>();
  for (const e of [...beside, ...[...runObservations.values()].flat()]) if (e.record.signal.kind === 'pushed') pushed.set(e.record.signal.commit, e.record.signal.at);

  const openRuns = [...merged.values()].filter((r) => !closes(runObservations.get(r.runId) ?? []));
  let head: string;
  let commits: BranchCommit[];
  try {
    head = await resolveCommit(checkout, `refs/heads/${branch}`);
    let from = lastTo;
    if (from === null) {
      // The first walk starts before the oldest merge still observed, so a revert of it already on the branch is found (D-R8a-10).
      const oldest = [...openRuns].sort((a, b) => a.mergedAt.localeCompare(b.mergedAt))[0];
      from = oldest === undefined ? head : ((await commitOf(checkout, oldest.mergeCommit)).parents[0] ?? null);
    }
    coverage.from = from;
    if (from !== null && !(await isAncestor(checkout, from, head))) {
      refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-rewritten', detail: `${from}, where the walk last stopped, is not an ancestor of ${branch}'s head ${head}` });
      return done();
    }
    commits = from === head ? [] : await firstParents(checkout, from, head);
  } catch (error) {
    refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
    return done();
  }

  try {
    // Changes already open precede the range; one inside it opens when the walk passes it.
    const inRange = new Set(commits.map((c) => c.commit));
    const open: OpenChange[] = [];
    const pending = new Map<string, OpenChange>();
    for (const r of openRuns) {
      const change: OpenChange = { change: r.mergeCommit, runId: r.runId, opened: { at: r.mergedAt, source: 'provider-reported' } };
      if (inRange.has(r.mergeCommit)) pending.set(r.mergeCommit, change);
      else open.push(change);
    }
    for (const o of openedBefore.values()) open.push({ change: o.change, runId: null, opened: { at: o.firstSeen, source: 'runtime' } });
    const closedRuns = new Set([...merged.values()].filter((r) => !openRuns.includes(r)).map((r) => r.mergeCommit));

    for (const commit of commits) {
      if (open.length > 0) {
        const diff: CommitDiff = await commitDiff(checkout, commit);
        for (const change of open) {
          change.inverse ??= await changeInverse(checkout, await commitOf(checkout, change.change));
          const found = classifyReversal(change.inverse, diff);
          if (found.kind === 'none') continue;
          const at = pushed.get(commit.commit) ?? { at: commit.committedAt, source: 'committer-claimed' as const };
          const signal: ObservationSignal = found.kind === 'rollback'
            ? { kind: 'rollback', commit: commit.commit, at, committedAt: commit.committedAt }
            : { kind: 'partial-reversal', commit: commit.commit, at, hunks: found.hunks, of: found.of };
          await vault.recordObservation({ runId: change.runId, change: change.change, signal, recordedAt: stamp(), collectedBy: 'runtime' });
          coverage.signals += 1;
        }
      }
      coverage.walked += 1;
      const run = pending.get(commit.commit);
      if (run !== undefined) open.push(run);
      else if (!merged.has(commit.commit) && !closedRuns.has(commit.commit) && !closedBaseline.has(commit.commit)) {
        // A change no run made: the baseline. Its window opens when the walk first sees it (D-R8a-09).
        const opened: OpenedChange = { change: commit.commit, firstSeen: stamp(), committedAt: commit.committedAt, leadTime: await leadTimeOf(checkout, commit) };
        coverage.opened.push(opened);
        open.push({ change: opened.change, runId: null, opened: { at: opened.firstSeen, source: 'runtime' } });
      }
    }
    coverage.to = head;

    // Windows close after every reversal is recorded (D-R8a-07).
    const firstSeen = new Map<string, OpenedChange>([...openedBefore, ...coverage.opened.map((o) => [o.change, o] as const)]);
    for (const change of open) {
      if (now() < Date.parse(change.opened.at) + windowMs) continue;
      const committedAt = (await commitOf(checkout, change.change)).committedAt;
      const record: ObservationRecord = {
        runId: change.runId, change: change.change, recordedAt: stamp(), collectedBy: 'runtime',
        signal: { kind: 'window-closed', at: { at: stamp(), source: 'runtime' }, windowMs, opened: change.opened, committedAt, leadTime: change.runId === null ? (firstSeen.get(change.change)?.leadTime ?? null) : null },
      };
      const sha = await vault.recordObservation(record);
      coverage.closed.push(change.change);
      if (change.runId !== null) {
        await vault.recordDecision({
          runId: change.runId, taskId: null, station: 'observe', decidedAt: stamp(), occurrence: randomUUID(),
          decision: { cause: 'observation-closed', decidedBy: 'observe', observation: sha, windowMs }, collectedBy: 'runtime',
        });
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    // The range is walked again next time; a reversal already recorded is the same signal, counted once (D-R8a-07).
    coverage.to = null;
    refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
  }
  try {
    await leaveObserve(vault, merged);
  } catch (error) {
    refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `a run whose window closed could not be moved out of observe: ${describe(error)}` });
  }
  return done();
}

/**
 * Every run whose window has closed and whose state still says it is being
 * watched is moved out, phase `exiting` at `observe` (D-R8a-04). Run after
 * every walk, so a close whose state commit failed is finished by the next.
 */
async function leaveObserve(vault: Vault, merged: ReadonlyMap<string, MergedRun>): Promise<void> {
  for (const run of merged.values()) {
    if (!closes(await vault.readObservations(run.runId))) continue;
    let state;
    try {
      state = await vault.readRunState(run.runId);
    } catch (error) {
      if (isNoRunState(error)) continue;
      throw error;
    }
    if (state.station !== 'observe' || state.phase !== 'working') continue;
    await vault.commitRunState({ ...state, phase: 'exiting' }, state.version);
  }
}

 import { approvalKey, gatedIterations } from '@olympus-ai/core';

 /** Reads one run's records. A run with no state is one refused at admission, or one with nothing yet. */
 export async function readRunRecords(vault: Vault, runId: RunId): Promise<RunRecords> {
   const decisions: EnforcementDecision[] = [];
   for (const ref of await vault.readDecisions(runId)) decisions.push(decode(await vault.read(ref)) as EnforcementDecision);
   decisions.sort((a, b) => a.decidedAt.localeCompare(b.decidedAt));
 
   // Integration before state: a merge happens only after its grant is committed, so a state read
   // after a merge was seen holds that grant, and a merge is never paired with an older state.
   const integration: IntegrationRecord[] = await readIntegration(vault, runId);
 
   let state: RunState;
   try {
     state = await vault.readRunState(runId);
   } catch (error) {
     // Only absence is absence; a state that cannot be read is a fault, never a run with no evidence (I5).
     if (!isNoRunState(error)) throw error;
     const refusal = decisions.find((d) => d.decision.cause === 'admission-refused');
     return {
       runId,
       ending: refusal === undefined ? 'unfinished' : 'refused',
       requestedLevel: null,
       stack: null,
       startedAt: refusal?.decidedAt ?? null,
       mergedAt: null,
       approvalsSpent: 0,
       tasks: [],
       decisions,
       usage: [],
       cost: null,
       reviewGrade: 'no-review',
       critiques: [],
+      observations: [],
     };
   }
 
   const admission = decode(await vault.read(state.admission)) as AdmissionRecord;
   const merged = integration.find((r) => r.kind === 'merged');
   const usage = await readUsage(vault, state);
 
   const bundles: EvidenceBundle[] = [];
   for (const ref of state.evidenceRefs) bundles.push(decode(await vault.read(ref)) as EvidenceBundle);
 
   const tasks = Object.entries(state.tasks).map(([id, status]): TaskRecord => {
     const taskId = id as TaskId;
     const own = bundles.filter((b) => b.taskId === taskId);
     // The line builds again only after a failed verdict, so every bundle before the last failed;
     // the last carries the task's status, the runtime's verdict and nothing else (I2).
     const attempts = own.map((b, i): AttemptRecord => ({
       outcome: i === own.length - 1 && status === 'passed' ? 'passed' : 'failed',
       claimMismatch: b.claimEvidenceDiff.length > 0,
     }));
     const calls = usage.filter((r) => r.taskId === taskId);
     const first = calls.find((r) => r.attempt === 1) ?? calls[0];
     const spent = state.attempts[taskId];
     return {
       taskId,
       station: calls[0]?.station ?? null,
       status,
       iterations: spent === undefined ? 0 : gatedIterations(spent),
       attempts,
       firstModel: first === undefined ? null : modelKey(first.model),
       models: [...new Set(calls.map((r) => modelKey(r.model)))].sort(),
     };
   });
 
   const ending: RunEnding = merged !== undefined
     ? 'merged'
     : state.cancelled !== null
       ? 'cancelled'
       : state.halted !== null
         ? 'halted'
         : state.approvals.some((g) => g.key === approvalKey('integrate', admission.run.requestedLevel) && g.usedAt !== null)
           // The grant for the last exit is spent in the commit that ends a passed run, as `runStanding` reads it (D-P9-08).
           ? 'passed'
           : Object.values(state.tasks).includes('parked') || Object.values(state.drafts).some((d) => d.parked)
           ? 'parked'
           : 'unfinished';
 
   return {
     runId,
     ending,
     requestedLevel: admission.run.requestedLevel,
     stack: recordedStack(admission),
     startedAt: admission.run.createdAt,
     mergedAt: merged?.kind === 'merged' ? merged.mergedAt : null,
     // The drive merges before it spends the integrate grant (D-I1b-01), so on a merged run that grant
     // was consumed by the merge, spent in state or not yet.
     approvalsSpent: state.approvals.filter((g) => g.usedAt !== null || (merged !== undefined && g.key === approvalKey('integrate', admission.run.requestedLevel))).length,
     tasks,
     decisions,
     usage,
     cost: costTotals(usage).run,
     reviewGrade: reviewGradeOf(state),
     critiques: await critiquesOf(vault, state),
+    observations: (await vault.readObservations(runId)).map((e) => e.record),
   };
 }

+/** When a closed change's window opened, from its `window-closed` record; null for any other record. */
+function openedAt(o: ObservationRecord): string | null {

-export function deriveOutcomes(runs: readonly RunRecords[], window: OutcomeWindow = {}): Outcomes {
+/** A signal's identity, so the same signal recorded twice — a walk run again, a delivery sent twice — counts once. */
+function signalKey(o: ObservationRecord): string {
+  const s = o.signal;
+  return JSON.stringify([o.change, s.kind, 'commit' in s ? s.commit : null, s.kind === 'check-failure' ? s.checkRun : null]);
+}

+interface ChangeOutcome {
+  readonly closed: boolean;
+  readonly failed: boolean;
+  readonly rollbacks: number;
+  readonly partials: number;
+  readonly checkFailures: number;
+  /** Merge to first counted rollback, from two times of one source; null without a counted rollback. */
+  readonly restore: { readonly source: 'provider-reported' | 'committer-claimed'; readonly ms: number } | null;
+}
+
+/**
+ * One change's outcome from its records (D-R8a-07): a signal counts only when
+ * recorded before the change's window closed. `merged` is the change's
+ * provider-reported landing time where one is known.
+ */
+function changeOutcome(records: readonly ObservationRecord[], merged: string | null): ChangeOutcome {
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  const unique = records.filter((o) => {
+    const key = signalKey(o);
+    if (seen.has(key)) return false;
+    seen.add(key);
+    return true;
+  });
+  const closedAt = unique.find((o) => o.signal.kind === 'window-closed');
+  const counted = closedAt === undefined ? [] : unique.filter((o) => o.signal.kind !== 'window-closed' && o.recordedAt <= closedAt.recordedAt);
+  const rollbacks = counted.filter((o) => o.signal.kind === 'rollback');
+  const checkFailures = counted.filter((o) => o.signal.kind === 'check-failure').length;
+  let restore: ChangeOutcome['restore'] = null;
+  const first = [...rollbacks].sort((a, b) => a.recordedAt.localeCompare(b.recordedAt))[0];
+  if (first?.signal.kind === 'rollback' && closedAt?.signal.kind === 'window-closed') {
+    restore = first.signal.at.source === 'provider-reported' && merged !== null
+      ? { source: 'provider-reported', ms: Date.parse(first.signal.at.at) - Date.parse(merged) }
+      : { source: 'committer-claimed', ms: Date.parse(first.signal.committedAt) - Date.parse(closedAt.signal.committedAt) };
+  }
+  return {
+    closed: closedAt !== undefined,
+    failed: rollbacks.length > 0 || checkFailures > 0,
+    rollbacks: rollbacks.length,
+    partials: counted.filter((o) => o.signal.kind === 'partial-reversal').length,
+    checkFailures,
+    restore,
+  };
+}
+
+function bySource(changes: readonly ChangeOutcome[]): BySource {
+  const of = (source: 'provider-reported' | 'committer-claimed'): Distribution =>
+    distribution(changes.flatMap((c) => (c.restore?.source === source ? [c.restore.ms] : [])));
+  return { providerReported: of('provider-reported'), committerClaimed: of('committer-claimed') };
+}
+
+/** The roles and seat identities whose calls built a run's change, read from its usage records (A-R9-01). */
+function builders(run: RunRecords): { roles: string[]; seats: string[] } {
+  const built = run.usage.filter((u) => u.station === 'build');
+  return {
+    roles: [...new Set(built.map((u) => u.role.id))].sort(),
+    seats: [...new Set(built.map((u) => `${u.role.id} ${u.driver ?? 'unrecorded'} ${modelKey(u.model)}`))].sort(),
+  };
+}
+
+/** Observation's figures, the line's from its runs and the baseline's from the records beside them. */
+function observedFigures(runs: readonly RunRecords[], baseline: BaselineRecords | null): {
+  safety: ObservedSafety; escaped: Proportion; timeToRestore: Beside<BySource>; leadTime: Outcomes['throughput']['baselineLeadTime'];
+} {
+  const merged = runs.filter((r) => r.ending === 'merged');
+  const outcomesOf = merged.map((r) => ({ run: r, outcome: changeOutcome(r.observations, r.mergedAt) }));
+  const closed = outcomesOf.filter((o) => o.outcome.closed);
+  const byRole = new Tally();
+  const bySeat = new Tally();
+  for (const { run, outcome } of closed) {
+    const { roles, seats } = builders(run);
+    for (const role of roles) byRole.add(role, outcome.failed);
+    for (const seat of seats) bySeat.add(seat, outcome.failed);
+  }
+  const perRun: Record<string, RunObservation> = {};
+  for (const { run, outcome } of outcomesOf) perRun[run.runId] = !outcome.closed ? 'observing' : outcome.failed ? 'failed' : 'clean';
+
+  // The baseline: every change a walk closed beside the runs, joined with the signals beside the runs on its commit.
+  const records = baseline?.observations ?? [];
+  const byChange = new Map<string, ObservationRecord[]>();
+  for (const o of records) byChange.set(o.change, [...(byChange.get(o.change) ?? []), o]);
+  const pushedAt = new Map<string, string>();
+  for (const o of records) if (o.signal.kind === 'pushed') pushedAt.set(o.signal.commit, o.signal.at.at);
+  const baseChanges: ChangeOutcome[] = [];
+  const lead: number[] = [];
+  let notMeasurable = 0;
+  let unattributed = 0;
+  for (const [change, own] of byChange) {
+    const closedAt = own.find((o) => o.signal.kind === 'window-closed');
+    if (closedAt === undefined) {
+      // A failed check on a commit no closed change carries: one still observing, or one no walk opened as a change.
+      unattributed += own.filter((o) => o.signal.kind === 'check-failure').length;
+      continue;
+    }
+    baseChanges.push(changeOutcome(own, pushedAt.get(change) ?? null));
+    if (closedAt.signal.kind === 'window-closed') {
+      const lt = closedAt.signal.leadTime;
+      if (lt?.kind === 'measured') lead.push(lt.ms);
+      else if (lt?.kind === 'not-measurable') notMeasurable += 1;
+    }
+  }
+  const sum = (xs: readonly ChangeOutcome[], f: (c: ChangeOutcome) => number): number => xs.reduce((n, c) => n + f(c), 0);
+  const own = closed.map((o) => o.outcome);
+  const failure = failureRate(own.filter((c) => c.failed).length, own.length);
+  return {
+    escaped: failure,
+    safety: {
+      observing: outcomesOf.length - closed.length,
+      changeFailure: { line: failure, baseline: failureRate(baseChanges.filter((c) => c.failed).length, baseChanges.length) },
+      changeFailureByRole: byRole.rates(failureRate),
+      changeFailureBySeat: bySeat.rates(failureRate),
+      rollbacks: { line: sum(own, (c) => c.rollbacks), baseline: sum(baseChanges, (c) => c.rollbacks) },
+      partialReversals: { line: sum(own, (c) => c.partials), baseline: sum(baseChanges, (c) => c.partials) },
+      checkFailures: { line: sum(own, (c) => c.checkFailures), baseline: sum(baseChanges, (c) => c.checkFailures) },
+      unattributed,
+      perRun,
+    },
+    timeToRestore: { line: bySource(own), baseline: bySource(baseChanges) },
+    leadTime: { committerClaimed: distribution(lead), notMeasurable },
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * The derivation itself, over records already read: pure, so a figure is a
+ * function of its records. `baseline` is the observations beside the runs;
+ * null where they were not read, so every baseline figure is `no-sample`.
+ */
+export function deriveOutcomes(runs: readonly RunRecords[], window: OutcomeWindow = {}, baseline: BaselineRecords | null = null): Outcomes {
   const population: Record<RunEnding, number> = { refused: 0, merged: 0, passed: 0, cancelled: 0, halted: 0, parked: 0, unfinished: 0 };
   for (const run of runs) population[run.ending] += 1;
 
   // Safety: every attempt, finished run or not — an attempt the runtime judged is a fact either way.
   let attempts = 0;
   let falseDone = 0;
   const fdStation = new Tally();
   const fdModel = new Tally();
   const fdLevel = new Tally();
   const fdStack = new Tally();
   const fdGrade = new Tally();
   for (const run of runs) {
     for (const task of run.tasks) {
       const model = task.models.length > 1 ? 'escalated' : (task.models[0] ?? 'no-call');
       for (const a of task.attempts) {
         const bad = a.outcome !== 'passed' || a.claimMismatch;
         attempts += 1;
         if (bad) falseDone += 1;
         fdStation.add(task.station ?? 'no-call', bad);
         fdModel.add(model, bad);
         fdLevel.add(`L${String(run.requestedLevel)}`, bad);
         fdStack.add(stackKey(run), bad);
         fdGrade.add(run.reviewGrade, bad);
       }
     }
   }
 
   // Throughput over admitted runs that finished; an unfinished run is counted in the population and nowhere else.
   const finished = runs.filter((r) => r.ending !== 'refused' && r.ending !== 'unfinished');
   const autonomous = (r: RunRecords): boolean => r.ending === 'merged' && r.approvalsSpent === 0;
   const byLevel = new Tally();
   const byStack = new Tally();
   const byGrade = new Tally();
   for (const r of finished) {
     byLevel.add(`L${String(r.requestedLevel)}`, autonomous(r));
     byStack.add(stackKey(r), autonomous(r));
     byGrade.add(r.reviewGrade, autonomous(r));
   }
   const critics = new Tally();
   for (const run of runs) for (const c of run.critiques) critics.add(c.grade, c.found);
 
   const fpStation = new Tally();
   const fpModel = new Tally();
   const fpStack = new Tally();
   const toGreen: number[] = [];
   const perTask: number[] = [];
   for (const run of runs) {
     for (const task of run.tasks) {
       const first = task.attempts[0];
       if (first === undefined) continue;
       fpStation.add(task.station ?? 'no-call', first.outcome === 'passed');
       fpModel.add(task.firstModel ?? 'no-call', first.outcome === 'passed');
       fpStack.add(stackKey(run), first.outcome === 'passed');
       perTask.push(task.attempts.length);
       if (task.status === 'passed') toGreen.push(task.iterations);
     }
   }
   const yields = fpStation.rates(successRate);
   const rolledYield = Object.keys(yields).length === 0 || Object.values(yields).some((p) => p.kind === 'no-sample')
     ? null
     : Object.values(yields).reduce((product, p) => product * (p.kind === 'rate' ? p.rate : 1), 1);
 
   let seated = 0;
   let returned = 0;
   const decisionsByCause: Record<string, number> = {};
   const refusalsByReason: Record<string, number> = {};
   const parksByCause: Record<string, number> = {};
   const waits = new Map<string, number[]>();
   const open: Record<string, number> = {};
   for (const run of runs) {
     // A run-level rate, so over finished runs: a review that has not returned is not a sample.
     if (finished.includes(run) && run.usage.some((u) => u.station === 'review')) {
       seated += 1;
       if (run.decisions.some((d) => d.station === 'review' && d.decision.cause === 'station-refused' && d.decision.refusal.reason !== 'approval-required')) returned += 1;
     }
     const requested = new Map<string, string[]>();
     for (const d of run.decisions) {
       bump(decisionsByCause, d.decision.cause);
       if (d.decision.cause === 'station-refused') {
         bump(refusalsByReason, d.decision.refusal.reason);
         if (d.decision.refusal.reason === 'parked') bump(parksByCause, d.decision.refusal.cause);
         if (d.decision.refusal.reason === 'approval-required') requested.set(d.decision.refusal.key, [...(requested.get(d.decision.refusal.key) ?? []), d.decidedAt]);
       }
     }
     // Each grant clears the first request of its key made before it; the requests repeated by resumes before that grant are the same wait.
     for (const [key, times] of requested) {
       const grants = run.decisions.filter((d) => d.decision.cause === 'approval-granted' && d.decision.key === key).map((d) => d.decidedAt);
       let from = 0;
       for (const granted of grants) {
         const first = times.slice(from).find((t) => t <= granted);
         if (first === undefined) continue;
         waits.set(key, [...(waits.get(key) ?? []), Date.parse(granted) - Date.parse(first)]);
         from = times.findIndex((t) => t > granted);
         if (from === -1) from = times.length;
       }
       if (from < times.length) bump(open, key);
     }
   }
 
   const merges = runs.filter((r) => r.ending === 'merged');
   const cycle = merges.flatMap((r) => (r.startedAt === null || r.mergedAt === null ? [] : [Date.parse(r.mergedAt) - Date.parse(r.startedAt)]));
   const spent = runs.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.cost?.metered.costUsd ?? 0), 0);
   const unknown = runs.some((r) => r.cost !== null && r.cost.bound === 'lower');
+  const observed = observedFigures(runs, baseline);
 
   return {
     window,
     population: { ...population, total: runs.length },
     safety: {
       caughtFalseDone: failureRate(falseDone, attempts),
       caughtFalseDoneByStation: fdStation.rates(failureRate),
       caughtFalseDoneByModel: fdModel.rates(failureRate),
       caughtFalseDoneByLevel: fdLevel.rates(failureRate),
       caughtFalseDoneByStack: fdStack.rates(failureRate),
       caughtFalseDoneByGrade: fdGrade.rates(failureRate),
-      escapedFalseDone: 'not-established',
+      escapedFalseDone: observed.escaped,
+      observed: observed.safety,
     },
     throughput: {
       autonomyRatio: successRate(finished.filter(autonomous).length, finished.length),
       autonomyRatioByLevel: byLevel.rates(successRate),
       autonomyRatioByStack: byStack.rates(successRate),
       autonomyRatioByGrade: byGrade.rates(successRate),
       criticFindingsByGrade: critics.rates(failureRate),
       firstPassYieldByStation: yields,
       firstPassYieldByModel: fpModel.rates(successRate),
       firstPassYieldByStack: fpStack.rates(successRate),
       rolledYield,
       iterationsToGreen: distribution(toGreen),
       attemptsPerTask: distribution(perTask),
       reviewExitRefused: failureRate(returned, seated),
       decisionsByCause,
       refusalsByReason,
       parksByCause,
       approvalWait: Object.fromEntries([...waits.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([k, v]) => [k, distribution(v)])),
       approvalsOpen: open,
       cycleTime: distribution(cycle),
       costPerMergedChange: merges.length === 0
         ? { kind: 'no-sample', spentUsd: spent }
         : { kind: 'cost', usd: spent / merges.length, spentUsd: spent, merges: merges.length, bound: unknown ? 'lower' : 'exact' },
+      timeToRestore: observed.timeToRestore,
+      baselineLeadTime: observed.leadTime,
     },
+    boundAssumption: BOUND_ASSUMPTION,
     notMeasured: NOT_MEASURED,
   };
 }

         if (again.ok || again.reason !== 'invalid-request') throw new Error(`P14: a second admission was ${again.ok ? 'admitted' : again.reason}`);
         const vault = (await rig.components()).vault;
         const d = await expectDecision(rig, vault, 'admission:invalid-request', { decidedBy: 'admission', taskId: null, station: null });
         if (d.decision.cause !== 'admission-refused' || d.decision.refusal.reason !== 'invalid-request') throw new Error('P14: unreachable');
         const problems = d.decision.refusal.problems;
         if (!problems.some((p) => p.path === 'runId' && p.code === 'already-admitted') || problems.some((p) => Object.hasOwn(p, 'message'))) {
           throw new Error(`P14: an invalid request was recorded with ${JSON.stringify(problems)}; each problem keeps its path and code and no message`);
         }
       });
     },
   },

     kind: 'scenario',

       // Admitted at L2 on the real composition, then resumed on the stubs: the resume repeats admission's check.

         }

   'resume:meter-lost': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: async () => {
       // A call whose sandbox could not be read stops the run with the reading recorded lost; the resume is refused for it (D-P13-20).
       await withLine('p14-meter-lost-', async (rig) => {
         const { resumeRun, startRun } = await api();
         const base = await rig.components();
         let destroyed = 0;
         const unreadable: SandboxProvider = {
           ...forwarding(base.sandbox),
           destroy: async (h) => {
             const teardown = await base.sandbox.destroy(h);
             destroyed += 1;
             // The base run's check sandbox is torn down first; the driver's call is the one whose meter is lost.
             if (destroyed === DRIVER_CALL) throw new Error("the relay's meter could not be read");
             return teardown;
           },
         };
         await startRun(await rig.request({ ...base, sandbox: unreadable })).then(
           () => { throw new Error('P14: a run whose meter could not be read was not stopped'); },
           (error: unknown) => { if (!String(error).includes('could not be destroyed')) throw error; },
         );
         const components = await rig.components();
         const outcome = await resumeRun({ runId: rig.runId, components });
         if (outcome.ok || outcome.reason !== 'meter-lost') throw new Error(`P14: a resume over a lost reading was ${outcome.ok ? 'driven' : outcome.reason}`);
         const d = await expectDecision(rig, components.vault, 'resume:meter-lost', { decidedBy: 'admission', taskId: null, station: null });
         if (d.decision.cause !== 'resume-refused' || d.decision.refusal.reason !== 'meter-lost' || !d.decision.refusal.tasks.includes(HELLO_TASK)) {
           throw new Error(`P14: a resume over a lost reading was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(d.decision)}`);
         }
       });
     },
   },
