   'station:gate-failed': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: async () => {
       const { STATION_CONTRACTS } = await import('@olympus-ai/core');
       await withLine('p14-gate-', async (rig) => {
         const { startRun } = await api();
         await writeManifest(rig.dirs, [{ id: 'always-fails', kind: 'compile', command: ['node', '-e', 'process.exit(1)'], required: true, timeoutMs: 10_000 }]);
         const driver = await stubDriver();
         const components = await rig.components({ driver, reviewer: driver });
         refusalOf(await startRun(await rig.request(components)), 'P14 gate');
         await expectDecision(rig, components.vault, 'station:gate-failed', { decidedBy: 'line', taskId: HELLO_TASK, station: 'verify' });
         // One decision per failed iteration, the last one's included, each naming the check and why it failed (R17, D-R17-06).
         const gates = (await decisionsOf(rig, components.vault)).filter((d) => keyOf(d.decision) === 'station:gate-failed');
         const { maxIterations } = STATION_CONTRACTS.build;
         for (const d of gates) {
           if (d.decision.cause !== 'station-refused' || d.decision.refusal.reason !== 'gate-failed' || d.decision.refusal.failed.length !== 1 || d.decision.refusal.failed[0]?.checkId !== 'always-fails' || d.decision.refusal.failed[0].cause !== 'exit-code') {
             throw new Error(`P14: a failed gate was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(d.decision)}`);
           }
         }
         if (gates.length !== maxIterations) throw new Error(`P14: ${String(maxIterations)} failed iterations recorded ${String(gates.length)} gate-failed decisions`);
       });
     },
   },

   'station:approval-required': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: async () => {
       await withLine('p14-required-', async (rig) => {
         const { runStanding } = await api();
         const components = await rig.components();
         await stoppedForApproval(rig, components);
         const d = await expectDecision(rig, components.vault, 'station:approval-required', { decidedBy: 'station-machine', taskId: null, station: 'integrate' });
         if (Number.isNaN(Date.parse(d.decidedAt))) throw new Error(`P14: approval-required was recorded with time ${d.decidedAt}`);
         // Reading where a run stands derives the same refusal and decides nothing.
         const before = (await components.vault.readDecisions(rig.runId)).length;
         await runStanding(components.vault, rig.runId);
         if ((await components.vault.readDecisions(rig.runId)).length !== before) throw new Error('P14: a status read recorded a decision');
       });
     },

   'station:parked:conflicts-exhausted': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: () => conflictScenario('conflicts-exhausted', (decisions) => {
       const parks = decisions.filter((d) => d.decision.cause === 'station-refused' && d.decision.refusal.reason === 'parked');
       const [park] = parks;
       if (parks.length !== 1 || park?.decision.decidedBy !== 'line' || park.taskId !== 'b' || park.station !== 'verify'
         || park.decision.cause !== 'station-refused' || park.decision.refusal.reason !== 'parked' || park.decision.refusal.cause !== 'conflicts-exhausted') {
         throw new Error(`P14: a conflict park was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(parks)}`);
       }
       // The conflict that parked it is recorded too, no later than the park. The Vault lists decisions by hash, so time orders them.
       const conflict = decisions.find((d) => d.decision.cause === 'task-conflict');
       if (conflict === undefined || conflict.decidedAt > park.decidedAt) throw new Error('P14: the park was recorded with no conflict before it');
     }),
   },

   'relay-refused': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: async () => {
       await withLine('p14-relay-', async (rig) => {
         const refusing: MeterReading = { kind: 'metered', calls: 3, inputTokens: 300, outputTokens: 30, cacheReadTokens: 0, cacheWriteTokens: 0, costUsd: 1, exhausted: 'cost', refused: 2 };
         const sandbox = await reportingProvider((n) => (n === DRIVER_CALL ? { meter: refusing } : {}));
         const components = await rig.components({ sandbox });
         await stoppedForApproval(rig, components);
         const [usage] = (await rig.state()).usage;
         const d = await expectDecision(rig, components.vault, 'relay-refused', { decidedBy: 'model-relay', taskId: HELLO_TASK, station: 'build' });
         if (d.decision.cause !== 'relay-refused' || JSON.stringify(d.decision.usage) !== JSON.stringify(usage) || d.decision.refused !== 2 || d.decision.exhausted !== 'cost') {
           throw new Error(`P14: the relay's refusals were recorded as ${JSON.stringify(d.decision)}`);
         }
         // A call whose relay refused nothing records no relay decision.
         const relayed = (await decisionsOf(rig, components.vault)).filter((x) => x.decision.cause === 'relay-refused');
         if (relayed.length !== 1) throw new Error(`P14: ${String(relayed.length)} relay decisions for one refusing call`);
       });
     },
   },
   'task-conflict': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: () => conflictScenario('task-conflict', (decisions) => {
       const found = decisions.filter((d) => d.decision.cause === 'task-conflict');
       const [d] = found;
       // Only the line may record one, which the Vault holds it to (A-R4-03).
       if (found.length !== 1 || d?.decision.cause !== 'task-conflict' || d.taskId !== 'b' || d.station !== 'verify'
         || JSON.stringify(d.decision.with) !== '["a"]' || JSON.stringify(d.decision.paths) !== '["parts/shared.txt"]') {
         throw new Error(`P14: a conflict was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(found)}`);
       }
     }),
   },
   'tier-escalated': {
     kind: 'scenario',
     run: async () => {
       await withLine('p14-escalate-', async (rig) => {
         const run = await failingRun(rig, { tier: 'fast', escalation: { afterFailedGates: 1, ceiling: 'standard' } });
         const vault = (await rig.components()).vault;
         const d = await expectDecision(rig, vault, 'tier-escalated', { decidedBy: 'line', taskId: HELLO_TASK, station: 'build' });
         if (d.decision.cause !== 'tier-escalated' || d.decision.from !== 'fast' || d.decision.to !== 'standard' || d.decision.failedGates !== 1 || run.decisions.length !== 1) {
           throw new Error(`P14: an escalation was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(d.decision)}, among ${String(run.decisions.length)}`);
         }
       });
     },
   },
 };
 
 /** The literals whose `reason` property is declared by `StationRefusal`, found in a package's own source. */
 /**
  * The string a literal's `reason` property is set to, however it is spelled:
  * a quoted key, and a literal inside parentheses, `as`, `satisfies`, or an
  * angle-bracket assertion, all count (external review of P14, codex-7).
  */

       id: 'I5.over-request-refused',

       run: async () => {
         const { StrictPolicyEngine } = await import('@olympus-ai/core');
         const engine = new StrictPolicyEngine();

         // Three bounds, three different values, so each refusal names the one
         // that actually bit. `build` is capped below the role ceiling, which is
         // itself below the global cap.
         const overRequests: Array<{ readonly level: AutonomyLevel; readonly station: StationId; readonly bound: string }> = [
           { level: 2, station: 'build', bound: `the ${String(BUILD_CAP)} cap on build` },

         ];
         for (const { level, station, bound } of overRequests) {
           const outcome = engine.resolveAutonomy(level, station, GRANTED_ROLE, policy);

               `I5: L${String(level)} at '${station}' was granted at L${String(outcome.level)} against ${bound}. ` +

           if (outcome.detail === '') {
             throw new Error(`I5: the refusal for L${String(level)} at '${station}' carries no detail`);

           throw new Error(`I5: the refusal does not name the bound that bit: ${global.detail}`);
         }
         if (!engine.resolveAutonomy(0, 'verify', GRANTED_ROLE, grounded).ok) {
           throw new Error('I5: L0 under a global cap of L0 was refused; the cap is a ceiling, not an exclusion');

         // The control: a request within every bound is granted, and granted at
         // the level requested rather than raised or lowered to a cap. An engine
         // that refused everything would satisfy the loop above.
         for (const level of [0, 1] as const) {
           const outcome = engine.resolveAutonomy(level, 'build', GRANTED_ROLE, policy);
           if (!outcome.ok) {
             throw new Error(`I5: L${String(level)} at 'build' is within every bound and was refused (${outcome.reason}: ${outcome.detail})`);
           }
           if (outcome.level !== level) {
             throw new Error(`I5: L${String(level)} at 'build' resolved to L${String(outcome.level)}; a request inside the caps is granted as asked, never adjusted`);
           }
         }
       },

     runtime({
       id: 'I5.malformed-document-refused-at-resolve',
       title:
         'resolvePolicy refuses a value that does not validate as a PolicyDocument, even though the parameter type says it is one, so a caller that asserts past the compiler cannot resolve a grant out of a malformed document; a genuine document still resolves',
       run: async () => {

         const document = grantingDocument();
         const scope = document.roles[GRANTED_ROLE];
         if (scope === undefined) throw new Error('I5: the fixture document defines no granted role');
 
         // Each of these is what a caller produces by writing `value as
         // PolicyDocument`. Without the guard, the first resolves a global cap
         // of 99 and grants every level; the rest widen a scope the document
         // never authorised.
         const rogues: Array<{ readonly what: string; readonly value: unknown }> = [
           { what: 'a global cap of 99', value: { ...document, globalCap: 99 } },
           { what: 'a role ceiling of 99', value: { ...document, roles: { [GRANTED_ROLE]: { ...scope, autonomyCeiling: 99 } } } },
           { what: 'a station id that is not one of the ten', value: { ...document, roles: { [GRANTED_ROLE]: { ...scope, stations: ['deploy'] } } } },
           { what: 'an unknown top-level key', value: { ...document, backdoor: true } },
         ];
         for (const { what, value } of rogues) {
           let refused = false;
           try {
             engine.resolvePolicy(value as never);
           } catch {
             refused = true;
           }
           if (!refused) {
             throw new Error(
               `I5: resolvePolicy accepted ${what}. The parameter type is a compiler claim, and a control that holds ` +
                 'only while every caller remembers to validate first is not closed.',
             );
           }
         }
 
         // The control: a genuine document resolves. A guard that threw on
         // everything would satisfy the loop above and break the engine.
         const policy = engine.resolvePolicy(document);
         if (policy.globalCap !== document.globalCap) {
           throw new Error(`I5: a valid document resolved to globalCap ${String(policy.globalCap)}, expected ${String(document.globalCap)}`);
         }
       },
     }),
     runtime({
       id: 'I5.malformed-autonomy-level-refused',
       title:
         'resolveAutonomy refuses a requested level that is not one of the four, rather than comparing it against the cap: NaN, a negative, and a fraction all survive a numeric comparison and would otherwise be answered with success carrying a level that is not a level',
       run: async () => {
         const { AUTONOMY_LEVELS, StrictPolicyEngine } = await import('@olympus-ai/core');
         const engine = new StrictPolicyEngine();
         const policy = engine.resolvePolicy(grantingDocument());
 
         for (const [label, level] of [
           ['NaN', Number.NaN], ['-1', -1], ['2.5', 2.5], ['4', 4],
         ] as const) {
           let refused = false;
           try {
             const outcome = engine.resolveAutonomy(level as never, 'verify', GRANTED_ROLE, policy);
             if (outcome.ok) {
               throw new Error(
                 `I5: resolveAutonomy answered ${label} with success at level ${String(outcome.level)}. ` +
                   'An unsupported input is a refusal, and success is the one answer a caller must never get for a ' +
                   'level nobody can honour.',
               );
             }
           } catch (error) {
             if (error instanceof Error && error.message.startsWith('I5:')) throw error;
             refused = true;
           }
           if (!refused) throw new Error(`I5: resolveAutonomy returned a refusal rather than refusing ${label} outright`);
         }
 
         // The control: every real level is still answered normally.
         for (const level of AUTONOMY_LEVELS) {
           const outcome = engine.resolveAutonomy(level, 'verify', GRANTED_ROLE, policy);
           const allowed = level <= ROLE_CEILING;
           if (outcome.ok !== allowed) {
             throw new Error(`I5: level ${String(level)} at 'verify' was ${outcome.ok ? 'granted' : 'refused'}, expected the opposite`);
           }
         }
       },
     }),
     runtime({
       id: 'I5.sandbox-egress-allowlist-enforced',
       title:
         'a sandbox provisioned with egress mode allowlist reaches the one host it names and no other, by name and by address; ' +
         'the direct route does not exist, so unsetting every proxy variable reaches nothing; an empty allow list is refused rather ' +
         'than read as deny-all or allow-all; and deny-all is unchanged, with --network none, no proxy, and no network created',
       run: async () => {
         await withProvider('i5-egress-allowlist-', async (provider, dirs) => {
           // An allowlist that names nothing is refused. It is not deny-all wearing the wrong
           // name, and it is not permission to reach everything; either reading would be the
           // silent degrade this invariant exists to refuse (D-P2-07, reversed by P10).
           const empty = await refusalFrom(() => provider.provision(specFor(dirs, 'rw', { egress: { mode: 'allowlist', allow: [] } })));
           if (empty.layer !== 'egress') {
             throw new Error(`I5: an empty allowlist was refused at the ${empty.layer} layer, not the egress layer`);
           }
 
           // deny-all is untouched by the allowlist existing: no proxy is started beside a
           // sandbox entitled to no egress, and no network is created for it.
           await withSandbox(provider, specFor(dirs), async (handle) => {
             const controls = provider.appliedControls(handle);
             if (controls.egress.mode !== 'deny-all') {
               throw new Error(`I5: a deny-all spec was applied as ${controls.egress.mode}`);
             }
             // `network: 'none'` is the type's guarantee once the mode is deny-all, so what is
             // read here is what Docker was actually told and what the container actually got.
             if (!controls.runArgs.join(' ').includes('--network none')) {
               throw new Error(`I5: a deny-all sandbox was not started with --network none: ${controls.runArgs.join(' ')}`);
             }
             const interfaces = await provider.exec(handle, ['ls', '/sys/class/net']);
             if (interfaces.stdout.trim().split(/\s+/).join(',') !== 'lo') {
               throw new Error(`I5: a deny-all sandbox has interfaces [${interfaces.stdout.trim()}]; expected loopback alone`);
             }
           });
 
           const name = originName();
           const handle = await provider.provision(specFor(dirs, 'rw', { egress: { mode: 'allowlist', allow: [name] } }));
           const { egress } = allowlistControls(provider, handle);
           try {
             await withOrigin(egress.proxy.outboundNetwork, name, async (origin) => {
               // The grant. The body proves the origin answered, not the proxy on its behalf.
               const reached = await provider.exec(handle, [
                 'sh', '-c', `wget -T 8 -O - http://${origin.name}:${String(ORIGIN_PORT)}/ 2>&1`,
               ]);
               if (!reached.stdout.includes(ORIGIN_BODY)) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: the allowlisted host was not reached: ${reached.stdout}${reached.stderr}`);
               }
 
               // Refused by name, and by address. The second is the one that matters: a refusal
               // that only ever fires on a name is a DNS failure wearing an allowlist's clothes.
               for (const host of [BLOCKED_NAME, BLOCKED_ADDRESS]) {
                 const answer = await throughProxy(provider, handle, `GET http://${host}/ HTTP/1.0`);
                 if (!answer.includes('403') || !answer.includes(host)) {
                   throw new Error(`I5: ${host} is not on the allowlist and was not refused by name in the answer: ${answer}`);
                 }
               }
 
               // The strongest form: the origin's own address, which the proxy demonstrably
               // reaches, refused for the one reason under test — the address is not on the list.
               const sameHost = await throughProxy(provider, handle, `GET http://${origin.address}:${String(ORIGIN_PORT)}/ HTTP/1.0`);
               if (!sameHost.includes('403') || sameHost.includes(ORIGIN_BODY)) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: a reachable host was allowed by address although only its name was granted: ${sameHost}`);
               }
 
               // There is nothing to bypass. The internal network carries no default route, so a
               // process that unsets every proxy variable is left with no second path to take.
               const routes = await provider.exec(handle, ['sh', '-c', 'ip route']);
               if (routes.stdout.includes('default')) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: the sandbox has a default route, so the proxy is a convention rather than the only way out: ${routes.stdout}`);
               }
               const bypass = await provider.exec(handle, [
                 'sh', '-c',
                 'unset http_proxy https_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY no_proxy NO_PROXY; ' +
                   `wget -T 4 -O - http://${origin.address}:${String(ORIGIN_PORT)}/ 2>&1`,
               ]);
               if (bypass.exitCode === 0 || bypass.stdout.includes(ORIGIN_BODY)) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: the allowlisted host was reached with every proxy variable unset: ${bypass.stdout}`);
               }
             });
           } finally {
             await provider.destroy(handle);
           }
 
           // The proxy is the sandbox's, and goes with it. One left running is a route off the
           // host with no workload behind it and nobody watching it.
           for (const [kind, leaked] of [
             ['container', egress.proxy.name],
             ['network', egress.proxy.internalNetwork],
             ['network', egress.proxy.outboundNetwork],
           ] as const) {
             if (await dockerHas(kind, leaked)) throw new Error(`I5: the ${kind} ${leaked} outlived the sandbox it served`);
           }
         });
       },
     }),
     STATION_MISSING_CAPABILITY_REFUSED,
     TASK_ATTEMPTS_ARE_BOUNDED,
     OVER_REQUEST_REFUSED_AT_ADMISSION,
     UNSUPPORTED_STACK_IS_LOUD,
     // P11: a provider with no probe is named, never trusted to observe the product from inside it.
     HTTP_PROBE_ABSENT_IS_NAMED,
     ADAPTER_REFUSAL_ENFORCED_AT_ADMISSION,
     CHECK_COMMAND_HAS_A_GRAMMAR,
     MISSING_CHECK_OR_SHRUNKEN_SUITE_REFUSES,
     WORKSPACE_IS_WRITABLE_BY_THE_TASK,
     // P13: what the relay cannot count, it charges or refuses; it never forwards it free.
     MODEL_RELAY_FAILS_CLOSED_ON_UNMETERED_USAGE,
     // R6: the same, under the openai-chat dialect (A-R6-01).
     MODEL_RELAY_FAILS_CLOSED_ON_UNMETERED_USAGE_OPENAI_CHAT,
     MODEL_RELAY_FAILS_CLOSED_ON_UNMETERED_USAGE_OPENAI_RESPONSES,
     // R21: http and no credential are each a declaration, never an inference (A-R21-01, A-R21-02).
     MODEL_RELAY_REFUSES_AN_UNDECLARED_LOCAL_ENDPOINT,
     // I1a: what the walking skeleton's own declaration stood in for, each now refusing on its own.
     UNSAFE_DECLARATION_SURVIVES_COMPOSITION,
     ADAPTER_REFUSAL_REFUSES_L3_END_TO_END,
     UNANALYSED_TESTS_ESCALATE_THE_APPROVAL,
     external({
       id: 'I5.unanalysed-tests-are-not-reported-clean',
       title:
         'an L2 run on the composed host over a repository with no vitest or jest stops at the integrate approval for the unanalysed suite, which escalates an exit that would otherwise advance; paid, one build and one review call',
       level: 'runtime',
       package: '@olympus-ai/api',
       file: 'test/paid/line.paid.test.ts',
     }),
     // R15: no default declaration, nothing to reproduce is refused, and a check that did not start is not a failure.
     BASE_DECLARATION_HAS_NO_DEFAULT,
     NOTHING_FAILING_ON_THE_BASE_IS_REFUSED,

 /** Wraps a Vault so a test can act between two of its calls; every method forwards. */
 export function around(inner: Vault, hooks: { beforeLock?: (by: string) => Promise<void>; beforeCommit?: (s: RunState) => void; afterCommit?: (count: number) => void }): Vault {
   let commits = 0;
   return {
     read: (ref) => inner.read(ref),
     lock: async (runId, paths, by): Promise<LockManifest> => {
       if (hooks.beforeLock !== undefined) await hooks.beforeLock(by);
       return inner.lock(runId, paths, by);
     },
     verifyLocks: (runId) => inner.verifyLocks(runId),
     writeEvidence: (b) => inner.writeEvidence(b),
     recordViolation: (v) => inner.recordViolation(v),
     recordAdmission: (a) => inner.recordAdmission(a),
     recordTaskResult: (runId, r) => inner.recordTaskResult(runId, r),
     recordUsage: (r) => inner.recordUsage(r),
     recordDecision: (d) => inner.recordDecision(d),
     readDecisions: (runId) => inner.readDecisions(runId),
     readUsage: (runId) => inner.readUsage(runId),
     recordIntegration: (r) => inner.recordIntegration(r),
     readIntegration: (runId) => inner.readIntegration(runId),
     recordBaseRun: (r) => inner.recordBaseRun(r),
     readBaseRun: (runId, draft) => inner.readBaseRun(runId, draft),
     recordIntent: (r) => inner.recordIntent(r),
     recordRubric: (r) => inner.recordRubric(r),
     recordDraft: (r) => inner.recordDraft(r),
     recordCriticResult: (r) => inner.recordCriticResult(r),
     recordDraftLock: (r) => inner.recordDraftLock(r),
     readLocks: (runId) => inner.readLocks(runId),
     listRuns: () => inner.listRuns(),
     recordTriggerDecision: (d, c) => inner.recordTriggerDecision(d, c),
     readTriggerDecisions: () => inner.readTriggerDecisions(),
     hasTriggerDecision: (source, key) => inner.hasTriggerDecision(source, key),
     readCandidate: (id) => inner.readCandidate(id),
     markCandidateAdmitted: (id, runId) => inner.markCandidateAdmitted(id, runId),
     markCandidateExpired: (id, at) => inner.markCandidateExpired(id, at),
     countRefusedDelivery: (bucket, hour, cause) => inner.countRefusedDelivery(bucket, hour, cause),
     readRefusedDeliveryCounts: () => inner.readRefusedDeliveryCounts(),
     recordNightly: (r) => inner.recordNightly(r),
     readNightly: () => inner.readNightly(),
+    recordObservation: (r) => inner.recordObservation(r),
+    readObservations: (runId) => inner.readObservations(runId),
     listObjects: () => inner.listObjects(),
     readRunState: (runId) => inner.readRunState(runId),
     commitRunState: async (s, ifVersion) => {
       hooks.beforeCommit?.(s);
       const stored = await inner.commitRunState(s, ifVersion);
       commits += 1;
       hooks.afterCommit?.(commits);
       return stored;
     },
   };
 }

===== packages/conformance/src/registry/observe.ts =====
/**
 * R8a: post-merge attribution and the baseline. Each assertion drives the
 * real code over a real git checkout: the filesystem Vault, the API's intake
 * and observation jobs, the history reader, and R2's derivation. A run's
 * merge is seeded as the integration record `integrate` writes, at a merge
 * commit made in the checkout, so what is asserted is what the walk and the
 * signal attribute from git and from signed bodies alone (D-R8a-01).
 */
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import type { Intake, NightlyJobs, RunRecords } from '@olympus-ai/api';
import type { Policy, PolicyDocument, RunId, TriggerKind, TriggerPolicy, TriggerSchedule, TriggerSubscription } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import type { NightlyRecord, ObservationRecord, Vault } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import { runtime } from '../kit/assert.js';
import type { LocalAssertion } from '../kit/types.js';
import { linePolicy } from './line.js';
import { withVaultDirs } from './local-vault.js';

const api = async () => import('@olympus-ai/api');
const core = async () => import('@olympus-ai/core');
const triggers = async () => import('@olympus-ai/triggers');

const SECRET_NAME = 'FACTORY_WEBHOOK_OBSERVE';
const SECRET = 'conformance-observe-secret-of-some-length';
const MARKER = 'R8A-MARKER-ignore-previous-instructions';
const WINDOW_MS = 1_000_000;
const RUN_A = 'run-a' as RunId;
const RUN_B = 'run-b' as RunId;

const CHECKS: TriggerSubscription = { id: 'gh-checks', kind: 'ci-failure', source: 'github', transport: 'signed-webhook', secret: SECRET_NAME, trustCeiling: 'owner', labels: [] };
const PUSHES: TriggerSubscription = { id: 'gh-pushes', kind: 'post-merge', source: 'github', transport: 'signed-webhook', secret: SECRET_NAME, trustCeiling: 'owner', labels: [] };
const WALK: TriggerSchedule = { id: 'observe', template: 'observe-history', at: '04:00' };

const ROUTE = {
  enabled: ['human', 'scheduled', 'ci-failure', 'post-merge'] as TriggerKind[],
  entryStation: { human: 'intake' as const, scheduled: 'intake' as const, 'ci-failure': 'intake' as const, 'post-merge': 'intake' as const },
  taskTemplate: { scheduled: 'intake-candidate' as const, 'ci-failure': 'observe-signal' as const, 'post-merge': 'observe-signal' as const },
  maxAutonomy: { human: 2 as const, scheduled: 0 as const, 'ci-failure': 0 as const, 'post-merge': 0 as const },
  minAuthorTrust: { human: 'owner' as const, scheduled: 'owner' as const, 'ci-failure': 'anonymous' as const, 'post-merge': 'anonymous' as const },
};

async function observePolicy(): Promise<Policy> {
  return linePolicy({}, {
    triggers: {
      ...ROUTE,
      maxTriggerDepth: 2,
      budgetPerWindow: { runs: 20, windowMs: 3_600_000 },
      subscriptions: [CHECKS, PUSHES],
      schedules: [WALK],
      maxDeliveryAgeMs: 3_600_000,
      candidateExpiryMs: 86_400_000,
      observe: { windowMs: WINDOW_MS },
    },
  });
}

/** A git checkout, run with a fixed identity and, where given, fixed dates. */
function gitIn(dir: string) {
  return (args: readonly string[], date?: string): string => execFileSync('git', ['-C', dir, '-c', 'user.email=r8a@example.invalid', '-c', 'user.name=r8a', '-c', 'core.autocrlf=false', ...args], {
    stdio: 'pipe', encoding: 'utf8',
    env: { ...process.env, ...(date === undefined ? {} : { GIT_AUTHOR_DATE: date, GIT_COMMITTER_DATE: date }) },
  }).trim();
}

const LINES = (n: number, change: Readonly<Record<number, string>> = {}): string => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => change[i + 1] ?? `line ${String(i + 1)}`).join('\n') + '\n';

interface Repo {
  readonly dir: string;
  readonly git: ReturnType<typeof gitIn>;
  /** Commits `files` on a branch off main and merges it with --no-ff, as GitHub's merge method does; returns the merge commit. */
  merge(branch: string, files: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, subject: string, date?: string): Promise<string>;
  /** Commits `files` straight onto main; returns the commit. */
  commit(files: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, message: string, date?: string): Promise<string>;
}

async function repo(dir: string): Promise<Repo> {
  await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
  const git = gitIn(dir);
  git(['init', '-q', '-b', 'main']);
  const write = async (files: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): Promise<void> => {
    for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(files)) await writeFile(join(dir, name), text);
    git(['add', '-A']);
  };
  await write({ 'a.txt': LINES(12), 'b.txt': 'b\n' });
  git(['commit', '-q', '-m', 'base'], '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z');
  return {
    dir, git,
    async merge(branch, files, subject, date) {
      git(['checkout', '-q', '-b', branch]);
      await write(files);
      git(['commit', '-q', '-m', `${subject} work`], date);
      git(['checkout', '-q', 'main']);
      git(['merge', '-q', '--no-ff', branch, '-m', subject], date);
      return git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']);
    },
    async commit(files, message, date) {
      await write(files);
      git(['commit', '-q', '-m', message], date);
      return git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']);
    },
  };
}

/** The record `integrate` writes once GitHub has merged a run's pull request (A-I1b-01). */
async function seedMerge(vault: Vault, runId: RunId, mergeCommit: string, mergedAt: string): Promise<void> {
  await vault.recordIntegration({
    runId, kind: 'merged', repository: 'octo/hello', baseBranch: 'main', baseCommit: mergeCommit, branch: `factory/${runId}`, commit: mergeCommit,
    pullRequest: 1, url: 'https://github.com/octo/hello/pull/1', collectedBy: 'runtime', mergeCommit, mergedAt,
  });
}

interface Rig {
  readonly vault: Vault;
  readonly repo: Repo;
  readonly jobs: NightlyJobs;
  readonly intake: Intake;
  readonly policy: Policy;
  /** The walk's clock, in ms; set before a firing to move it. */
  readonly clock: { now: number };
}

async function withObserve<T>(prefix: string, body: (rig: Rig) => Promise<T>, target: 'checkout' | 'missing' = 'checkout'): Promise<T> {
  const { Intake: IntakeClass, NightlyJobs: Jobs, SCANNERS } = await api();
  const { LocalVault } = await import('@olympus-ai/vault');
  return withVaultDirs(prefix, async (dirs, base) => {
    const vault = new LocalVault(dirs);
    const r = await repo(join(base, 'target'));
    const clock = { now: Date.now() };
    const jobs = new Jobs({
      vault, sandbox: unusedSandbox(), workspaces: { root: join(base, 'trees'), user: { uid: 1000, gid: 1000 } }, limits: null, scanners: SCANNERS,
      target: { checkout: target === 'checkout' ? r.dir : join(base, 'no-such-checkout'), branch: 'main' }, now: () => clock.now,
    });
    const intake = new IntakeClass({ vault, secrets: (name) => (name === SECRET_NAME ? SECRET : undefined), jobs });
    return body({ vault, repo: r, jobs, intake, policy: await observePolicy(), clock });
  });
}

/** The walk provisions no sandbox; one that is asked for fails the assertion. */
function unusedSandbox() {
  const fail = (): never => {
    throw new Error('R8a: the history walk asked for a sandbox');
  };
  return { id: 'unused', provision: fail, exec: fail, destroy: fail, capabilities: () => ({ computerUse: false, gpu: false, os: 'linux' as const, persistent: false, remote: false }) };
}

async function walkOn(rig: Rig, date: string): Promise<NightlyRecord> {
  await rig.intake.fire(rig.policy, WALK, date);
  const entry = (await rig.vault.readNightly()).find((e) => e.record.schedule === WALK.id && e.record.date === date);
  if (entry === undefined) throw new Error(`R8a: no walk record for ${date}`);
  return entry.record;
}

const kinds = (records: ReadonlyArray<{ record: ObservationRecord }>, kind: ObservationRecord['signal']['kind']): ObservationRecord[] =>
  records.map((e) => e.record).filter((r) => r.signal.kind === kind);

async function signed(rig: Rig, subscription: TriggerSubscription, payload: unknown): Promise<string> {
  const { signBody } = await triggers();
  const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(payload));
  const outcome = await rig.intake.deliver(rig.policy, subscription, signBody(body, SECRET), body);
  return outcome.outcome;
}

function checkFailure(sha: string, extra: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> = {}): unknown {
  return {
    action: 'completed',
    check_run: { id: 4242, head_sha: sha, conclusion: 'failure', completed_at: new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d+Z$/u, 'Z'), name: `${MARKER} lint`, output: { title: MARKER, summary: MARKER }, ...extra },
    repository: { full_name: 'octo/hello' },
  };
}

function push(after: string, pushedAt: number): unknown {
  return { ref: 'refs/heads/main', after, head_commit: { message: MARKER }, repository: { full_name: 'octo/hello', default_branch: 'main', pushed_at: pushedAt } };
}

/** A merged run's records as R2 reads them, with the observations the store holds for it. */
async function mergedRun(vault: Vault, runId: RunId, mergedAt: string): Promise<RunRecords> {
  return {
    runId, ending: 'merged', requestedLevel: 2, stack: 'vitest@4', startedAt: mergedAt, mergedAt, approvalsSpent: 1,
    tasks: [], decisions: [], usage: [], cost: null, reviewGrade: 'different-family', critiques: [],
    observations: (await vault.readObservations(runId)).map((e) => e.record),
  };
}

const ago = (ms: number): string => new Date(Date.now() - ms).toISOString();

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const ROLLBACK_BY_DIFF_ALONE = runtime({
  id: 'I2.observe-rollback-is-found-by-diff-alone',
  title: "a revert whose message names another run's change is attributed to the change its diff inverts, and a commit titled \"Revert\" whose diff inverts nothing is attributed to none",
  run: async () => {
    await withObserve('r8a-diff-', async (rig) => {
      const a = await rig.repo.merge('feature-a', { 'a.txt': LINES(12, { 2: 'two, changed by a' }), 'c.txt': 'c\n' }, 'Add c');
      const b = await rig.repo.merge('feature-b', { 'd.txt': 'd\n' }, 'Add d');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_A, a, ago(10_000));
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_B, b, ago(10_000));
      // Titled as a revert of A, naming A's commit, and inverting nothing.
      const decoy = await rig.repo.commit({ 'b.txt': 'b, edited\n' }, `Revert "Add c"\n\nThis reverts commit ${a}.`);
      // A's real inverse, whose message names B.
      rig.repo.git(['revert', '-m', '1', '--no-edit', a]);
      rig.repo.git(['commit', '-q', '--amend', '-m', `Revert "Add d"\n\nThis reverts commit ${b}.`]);
      const revert = rig.repo.git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']);
      const record = await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-05');
      if (record.outcome.kind !== 'completed') throw new Error(`I2: the walk was refused: ${JSON.stringify(record.outcome)}`);
      const onA = kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A), 'rollback');
      const onB = await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_B);
      if (onA.length !== 1 || onA[0]?.signal.kind !== 'rollback' || onA[0].signal.commit !== revert) throw new Error(`I2: A's rollback by diff was not found: ${JSON.stringify(onA)}`);
      if (onB.some((e) => e.record.signal.kind === 'rollback' || e.record.signal.kind === 'partial-reversal')) throw new Error('I2: a revert message naming B attributed a rollback to B');
      const all = [...(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A)), ...onB, ...(await rig.vault.readObservations(null))];
      if (all.some((e) => 'commit' in e.record.signal && e.record.signal.commit === decoy)) throw new Error('I2: a commit titled "Revert" that inverts nothing was attributed');
    });
  },
});

const PARTIAL_REPORTED_APART = runtime({
  id: 'I5.observe-partial-reversal-is-reported-apart',
  title: "a commit that inverts some of a change's hunks and not all is a partial reversal, reported on its own line and never counted as a rollback or a failure",
  run: async () => {
    const { deriveOutcomes } = await api();
    await withObserve('r8a-partial-', async (rig) => {
      const mergedAt = ago(2 * WINDOW_MS);
      const a = await rig.repo.merge('feature-a', { 'a.txt': LINES(12, { 2: 'two, changed', 10: 'ten, changed' }) }, 'Edit a twice');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_A, a, mergedAt);
      const partial = await rig.repo.commit({ 'a.txt': LINES(12, { 10: 'ten, changed' }) }, 'tidy line two');
      await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-05');
      const own = await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A);
      const found = kinds(own, 'partial-reversal');
      if (found.length !== 1 || found[0]?.signal.kind !== 'partial-reversal' || found[0].signal.commit !== partial || found[0].signal.hunks !== 1 || found[0].signal.of !== 2) {
        throw new Error(`I5: the partial reversal was not reported as one of two hunks: ${JSON.stringify(found)}`);
      }
      if (kinds(own, 'rollback').length > 0) throw new Error('I5: a partial reversal was recorded as a rollback');
      const o = deriveOutcomes([await mergedRun(rig.vault, RUN_A, mergedAt)]);
      if (o.safety.observed.partialReversals.line !== 1 || o.safety.observed.rollbacks.line !== 0) throw new Error(`I5: the counts are ${JSON.stringify(o.safety.observed)}`);
      if (o.safety.escapedFalseDone.kind !== 'rate' || o.safety.escapedFalseDone.count !== 0) throw new Error('I5: a partial reversal was counted as an escaped false-done');
    });
  },
});

const SIGNAL_BY_COMMIT_ALONE = runtime({
  id: 'I7.observe-signal-is-attributed-by-commit-alone',
  title: "a failed check is recorded against the run whose merge commit it names whatever its other fields say, a commit no run merged is recorded unattributed, and no candidate is written",
  run: async () => {
    await withObserve('r8a-signal-', async (rig) => {
      const a = await rig.repo.merge('feature-a', { 'c.txt': 'c\n' }, 'Add c');
      const b = await rig.repo.merge('feature-b', { 'd.txt': 'd\n' }, 'Add d');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_A, a, ago(10_000));
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_B, b, ago(10_000));
      // Every field but the commit names B.
      const answered = await signed(rig, CHECKS, checkFailure(a, { external_id: RUN_B, check_suite: { head_branch: `factory/${RUN_B}`, head_sha: b }, pull_requests: [{ number: 2, head: { sha: b } }] }));
      if (answered !== 'observed') throw new Error(`I7: the signal was answered ${answered}`);
      const onA = kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A), 'check-failure');
      if (onA.length !== 1 || onA[0]?.signal.kind !== 'check-failure' || onA[0].signal.checkRun !== '4242') throw new Error(`I7: the failure was not recorded against A by its commit: ${JSON.stringify(onA)}`);
      if ((await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_B)).length > 0) throw new Error("I7: fields naming B attributed a signal to B");
      const stray = await rig.repo.commit({ 'b.txt': 'b, straight onto main\n' }, 'direct');
      await signed(rig, CHECKS, checkFailure(stray));
      const beside = kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(null), 'check-failure');
      if (beside.length !== 1 || beside[0]?.runId !== null || beside[0].change !== stray) throw new Error(`I7: a commit no run merged was not recorded unattributed: ${JSON.stringify(beside)}`);
      const entries = await rig.vault.readTriggerDecisions();
      if (entries.some((e) => e.candidate !== null)) throw new Error('I7: a signal wrote a candidate');
      if (!entries.every((e) => e.decision.outcome.kind === 'observed')) throw new Error(`I7: a signal's decision is ${JSON.stringify(entries.map((e) => e.decision.outcome))}`);
    });
  },
});

const TEMPLATES_ON_THEIR_KINDS = runtime({
  id: 'I7.observe-templates-are-valid-only-on-their-kinds',
  title: 'observe-signal validates on post-merge and ci-failure alone and records only extracted fields; observe-history validates on scheduled alone',
  run: async () => {
    const { DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT, validatePolicyDocument } = await core();
    const doc = (t: Partial<TriggerPolicy>): PolicyDocument => ({
      ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT,
      triggers: { ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT.triggers, ...ROUTE, maxDeliveryAgeMs: 3_600_000, candidateExpiryMs: 86_400_000, observe: { windowMs: WINDOW_MS }, ...t },
    });
    const valid = validatePolicyDocument(doc({ schedules: [WALK] }));
    if (!valid.ok) throw new Error(`I7: the whole observe route was refused: ${JSON.stringify(valid.defects)}`);
    const issueRoute = {
      enabled: [...ROUTE.enabled, 'issue'] as TriggerKind[],
      entryStation: { ...ROUTE.entryStation, issue: 'intake' as const },
      maxAutonomy: { ...ROUTE.maxAutonomy, issue: 0 as const },
      minAuthorTrust: { ...ROUTE.minAuthorTrust, issue: 'owner' as const },
    };
    const refusedAt = (t: Partial<TriggerPolicy>, path: string, what: string): void => {
      const out = validatePolicyDocument(doc(t));
      if (out.ok || !out.defects.some((d) => d.path === path)) throw new Error(`I7: ${what} validated, or was refused elsewhere: ${JSON.stringify(out.ok ? [] : out.defects)}`);
    };
    refusedAt({ ...issueRoute, taskTemplate: { ...ROUTE.taskTemplate, issue: 'observe-signal' } }, 'triggers.taskTemplate.issue', 'observe-signal on issue');
    refusedAt({ taskTemplate: { ...ROUTE.taskTemplate, scheduled: 'observe-signal' } }, 'triggers.taskTemplate.scheduled', 'observe-signal on scheduled');
    refusedAt({ schedules: [{ id: 'sig', template: 'observe-signal', at: '05:00' }] }, 'triggers.schedules[0].template', 'a schedule naming observe-signal');
    refusedAt({ taskTemplate: { ...ROUTE.taskTemplate, 'ci-failure': 'observe-history' } }, 'triggers.taskTemplate.ci-failure', 'observe-history on ci-failure');
    refusedAt({ taskTemplate: { ...ROUTE.taskTemplate, 'post-merge': 'observe-history' } }, 'triggers.taskTemplate.post-merge', 'observe-history on post-merge');

    // What a signal records is the extracted fields: nothing else the body carries reaches the record.
    await withObserve('r8a-fields-', async (rig) => {
      const a = await rig.repo.merge('feature-a', { 'c.txt': 'c\n' }, 'Add c');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_A, a, ago(10_000));
      await signed(rig, CHECKS, checkFailure(a));
      await signed(rig, PUSHES, push(a, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)));
      const stored = JSON.stringify(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A));
      if (stored.includes(MARKER)) throw new Error('I7: text outside the extracted fields reached an observation');
      if (kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A), 'pushed').length !== 1) throw new Error('I7: the push was not recorded as a provider-reported landing');
    });
  },
});

const UNREADABLE_HISTORY_REFUSED = runtime({
  id: 'I5.observe-unreadable-history-is-refused',
  title: 'a checkout the walk cannot read, and a recorded head the branch no longer descends from, are each a refusal naming its cause and never an empty walk',
  run: async () => {
    await withObserve('r8a-missing-', async (rig) => {
      const record = await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-05');
      if (record.outcome.kind !== 'refused' || !record.outcome.refusals.some((r) => r.part === 'history' && r.cause === 'history-unreadable')) {
        throw new Error(`I5: an unreadable checkout was ${JSON.stringify(record.outcome)}`);
      }
      if (record.observation?.to !== null) throw new Error('I5: an unreadable checkout recorded a head it walked to');
    }, 'missing');
    await withObserve('r8a-rewritten-', async (rig) => {
      await rig.repo.commit({ 'b.txt': 'b, one\n' }, 'one');
      const first = await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-05');
      if (first.outcome.kind !== 'completed') throw new Error(`I5: the first walk was refused: ${JSON.stringify(first.outcome)}`);
      // The branch is rewritten under the head the walk recorded.
      rig.repo.git(['reset', '-q', '--hard', 'HEAD~1']);
      await rig.repo.commit({ 'b.txt': 'b, rewritten\n' }, 'rewritten');
      const second = await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-06');
      if (second.outcome.kind !== 'refused' || !second.outcome.refusals.some((r) => r.cause === 'history-rewritten')) throw new Error(`I5: a rewritten range was ${JSON.stringify(second.outcome)}`);
      if (second.observation?.to !== null || second.observation.walked !== 0) throw new Error('I5: a rewritten range was recorded as walked');
    });
  },
});

export const OPEN_WINDOW_IN_NO_DENOMINATOR = runtime({
  id: 'I5.observe-open-window-is-in-no-denominator',
  title: "a run whose window has not closed is in no denominator, even with a rollback recorded, and is counted as observing; one whose window passed is moved out of observe with P14's record",
  run: async () => {
    const { deriveOutcomes } = await api();
    await withObserve('r8a-open-', async (rig) => {
      const oldMerge = ago(2 * WINDOW_MS);
      const freshMerge = ago(10_000);
      const a = await rig.repo.merge('feature-a', { 'c.txt': 'c\n' }, 'Add c');
      const b = await rig.repo.merge('feature-b', { 'd.txt': 'd\n' }, 'Add d');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_A, a, oldMerge);
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_B, b, freshMerge);
      rig.repo.git(['revert', '-m', '1', '--no-edit', b]);
      await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-05');
      if (kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_B), 'rollback').length !== 1) throw new Error("I5: B's rollback was not recorded");
      if (kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_B), 'window-closed').length !== 0) throw new Error("I5: B's window closed before it passed");
      if (kinds(await rig.vault.readObservations(RUN_A), 'window-closed').length !== 1) throw new Error("I5: A's window did not close once it passed");
      const decisions = await Promise.all((await rig.vault.readDecisions(RUN_A)).map(async (ref) => JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(await rig.vault.read(ref))) as { decision: { cause: string; decidedBy: string } }));
      if (!decisions.some((d) => d.decision.cause === 'observation-closed' && d.decision.decidedBy === 'observe')) throw new Error("I5: A's window closed with no record in P14's");
      const o = deriveOutcomes([await mergedRun(rig.vault, RUN_A, oldMerge), await mergedRun(rig.vault, RUN_B, freshMerge)]);
      const escaped = o.safety.escapedFalseDone;
      if (escaped.kind !== 'rate' || escaped.sample !== 1 || escaped.count !== 0) throw new Error(`I5: an open window entered a denominator: ${JSON.stringify(escaped)}`);
      if (o.safety.observed.observing !== 1 || o.safety.observed.perRun[RUN_B] !== 'observing') throw new Error(`I5: B is not counted as observing: ${JSON.stringify(o.safety.observed)}`);
    });
  },
});

const WINDOW_REQUIRED = runtime({
  id: 'I5.observe-window-is-required',
  title: 'a policy that names observe-signal or observe-history with no observation window is refused, and no window is implied',
  run: async () => {
    const { DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT, validatePolicyDocument } = await core();
    const base = { ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT.triggers, ...ROUTE, maxDeliveryAgeMs: 3_600_000, candidateExpiryMs: 86_400_000 };
    const signalOnly = validatePolicyDocument({ ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT, triggers: base });
    if (signalOnly.ok || !signalOnly.defects.some((d) => d.path === 'triggers.observe')) throw new Error('I5: observe-signal with no window validated');
    const walkOnly = validatePolicyDocument({ ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT, triggers: { ...base, taskTemplate: { scheduled: 'intake-candidate' }, enabled: ['human', 'scheduled'], schedules: [WALK] } });
    if (walkOnly.ok || !walkOnly.defects.some((d) => d.path === 'triggers.observe')) throw new Error('I5: observe-history with no window validated');
    const zero = validatePolicyDocument({ ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT, triggers: { ...base, observe: { windowMs: 0 } } });
    if (zero.ok) throw new Error('I5: a window of zero validated');
    const whole = validatePolicyDocument({ ...DEFAULT_POLICY_DOCUMENT, triggers: { ...base, observe: { windowMs: WINDOW_MS } } });
    if (!whole.ok) throw new Error(`I5: a policy with its window was refused: ${JSON.stringify(whole.defects)}`);
  },
});

const RATES_BOUND_AND_BASELINE = runtime({
  id: 'I2.observe-rates-carry-bound-and-baseline',
  title: "R2's escaped rate carries its sample and bound, the baseline sits beside each rate, time to restore keeps provider-reported and committer-claimed times apart, and a one-parent change's lead time is not measurable",
  run: async () => {
    const { deriveOutcomes, outcomes } = await api();
    await withObserve('r8a-rates-', async (rig) => {
      const mergedAt = ago(2 * WINDOW_MS);
      const m = await rig.repo.merge('feature-a', { 'c.txt': 'c\n' }, 'Add c', '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_A, m, mergedAt);
      const mb = await rig.repo.merge('feature-b', { 'g.txt': 'g\n' }, 'Add g', '2026-10-01T00:30:00Z');
      await seedMerge(rig.vault, RUN_B, mb, mergedAt);
      const direct = await rig.repo.commit({ 'e.txt': 'e\n' }, 'Add e', '2026-10-01T01:00:00Z');
      await rig.repo.merge('feature-f', { 'f.txt': 'f\n' }, 'Add f', '2026-10-01T02:00:00Z');
      rig.repo.git(['revert', '-m', '1', '--no-edit', m], '2026-10-01T03:00:00Z');
      const rollback = rig.repo.git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']);
      rig.repo.git(['revert', '--no-edit', direct], '2026-10-01T04:00:00Z');
      // B's rollback has no reported push, so both ends of its time to restore are commit dates.
      rig.repo.git(['revert', '-m', '1', '--no-edit', mb], '2026-10-01T05:00:00Z');
      // The run's rollback landed by a push GitHub reported; the baseline's did not.
      const pushedAt = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      await signed(rig, PUSHES, push(rollback, pushedAt));
      await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-05');
      rig.clock.now += WINDOW_MS + 1;
      await walkOn(rig, '2026-11-06');

      const runs = [await mergedRun(rig.vault, RUN_A, mergedAt), await mergedRun(rig.vault, RUN_B, mergedAt)];
      const o = deriveOutcomes(runs, {}, { observations: (await rig.vault.readObservations(null)).map((e) => e.record) });
      const escaped = o.safety.escapedFalseDone;
      if (escaped.kind !== 'rate' || escaped.count !== 2 || escaped.sample !== 2 || escaped.bound.side !== 'upper' || !(escaped.bound.value > 0)) {
        throw new Error(`I2: the escaped rate carries no sample and bound: ${JSON.stringify(escaped)}`);
      }
      const base = o.safety.observed.changeFailure.baseline;
      if (base.kind !== 'rate' || base.count !== 1 || base.sample !== 5) throw new Error(`I2: the baseline is not beside the rate: ${JSON.stringify(o.safety.observed.changeFailure)}`);
      const own = o.throughput.timeToRestore.line;
      const provider = own.providerReported;
      const committer = own.committerClaimed;
      if (provider.kind !== 'distribution' || provider.sample !== 1 || provider.median !== pushedAt * 1000 - Date.parse(mergedAt)
        || committer.kind !== 'distribution' || committer.sample !== 1 || committer.median !== 4.5 * 3_600_000) {
        throw new Error(`I2: the line's time to restore combines sources: ${JSON.stringify(own)}`);
      }
      const theirs = o.throughput.timeToRestore.baseline;
      if (theirs.committerClaimed.kind !== 'distribution' || theirs.committerClaimed.median !== 3 * 3_600_000 || theirs.providerReported.kind !== 'no-sample') {
        throw new Error(`I2: the baseline's time to restore combines sources: ${JSON.stringify(theirs)}`);
      }
      const lead = o.throughput.baselineLeadTime;
      if (lead.committerClaimed.kind !== 'distribution' || lead.committerClaimed.sample !== 1 || lead.notMeasurable !== 4) throw new Error(`I2: lead time is ${JSON.stringify(lead)}`);
      if (!o.boundAssumption.includes('not independent')) throw new Error('I2: the report does not say its bounds assume independent runs');
      // The store-wide report reads the baseline beside the runs too.
      const whole = await outcomes(rig.vault);
      if (whole.safety.observed.changeFailure.baseline.kind !== 'rate') throw new Error('I2: the report read no baseline');
    });
  },
});

 /**
  * What the trigger policy means together (D-A-TF-03, the relational check
  * D-P3-13 left to admission until a registry existed). A non-human kind in
  * `enabled` needs its whole route; a subscription or schedule needs its kind
  * enabled; ids are unique. `human` keeps D-P3-05's route: an entry station,
  * a cap, and a floor, and no template.
  */
 function routeDefects(triggers: TriggerPolicy, path: string, defects: PolicyDefect[]): void {
   const nonHuman = triggers.enabled.filter((kind) => kind !== 'human');
   for (const kind of nonHuman) {
     const missing: string[] = [];
     if (!Object.hasOwn(triggers.entryStation, kind)) missing.push('an entryStation');
     if (!Object.hasOwn(triggers.taskTemplate, kind)) missing.push('a registered taskTemplate');
     if (!Object.hasOwn(triggers.maxAutonomy, kind)) missing.push('a maxAutonomy');
     if (!Object.hasOwn(triggers.minAuthorTrust, kind)) missing.push('a minAuthorTrust');
     if (!hasFieldSchema(kind)) missing.push('a field schema in code, which this kind does not have');
     if (missing.length > 0) {
       defects.push({ path: child(path, 'enabled'), problem: `'${kind}' is enabled without ${missing.join(', ')}; a non-human kind is enabled only with its whole route` });
     }
     // The one registered template records a candidate at intake, and a run admitted from it starts there; any
     // other station would validate and route nothing (codex-5). A template that enters elsewhere widens this.
     const station = Object.hasOwn(triggers.entryStation, kind) ? triggers.entryStation[kind] : undefined;
     if (station !== undefined && station !== 'intake') {
       defects.push({ path: child(child(path, 'entryStation'), kind), problem: `'${kind}' enters at '${station}', but every registered template enters at intake` });
     }
   }
   if (nonHuman.length > 0) {
     for (const key of ['maxDeliveryAgeMs', 'candidateExpiryMs'] as const) {
       if (triggers[key] === undefined) defects.push({ path: child(path, key), problem: 'required while a non-human kind is enabled' });
     }
   }
   const unique = (ids: readonly string[], at: string): void => {
     const seen = new Set<string>();
     for (const id of ids) {
       if (seen.has(id)) defects.push({ path: at, problem: `the id '${id}' appears more than once` });
       seen.add(id);
     }
   };
   const subscriptions = triggers.subscriptions ?? [];
   const schedules = triggers.schedules ?? [];
   unique(subscriptions.map((s) => s.id), child(path, 'subscriptions'));
   unique(schedules.map((s) => s.id), child(path, 'schedules'));
   subscriptions.forEach((s, i) => {
     if (!triggers.enabled.includes(s.kind)) {
       defects.push({ path: `${child(path, 'subscriptions')}[${String(i)}].kind`, problem: `'${s.kind}' is not enabled, so this subscription grants nothing` });
     }
   });
   if (schedules.length > 0 && !triggers.enabled.includes('scheduled')) {
     defects.push({ path: child(path, 'schedules'), problem: `'scheduled' is not enabled, so no schedule fires` });
   }
   // A job template runs a job in place of recording an event, so only a schedule's firing can name one (A-R13a-01).
   for (const [kind, template] of Object.entries(triggers.taskTemplate)) {
     if (kind !== 'scheduled' && isJobTemplate(template)) {
       defects.push({ path: child(child(path, 'taskTemplate'), kind), problem: `'${template}' is a job template, valid only on 'scheduled'` });
     }
+    // A signal is attributed by the commit its event names, so only the two kinds that name one can carry it (D-A-OB-02).
+    if (isSignalTemplate(template) && !SIGNAL_KINDS.some((k) => k === kind)) {
+      defects.push({ path: child(child(path, 'taskTemplate'), kind), problem: `'${template}' is valid only on ${SIGNAL_KINDS.map((k) => `'${k}'`).join(' and ')}` });
+    }
+  }
+  // An observe template with no window would never close one, or close it at once; neither is implied (I5, D-R8a-05).
+  const observing = Object.values(triggers.taskTemplate).some(isObserveTemplate) || schedules.some((s) => isObserveTemplate(s.template));
+  if (observing && triggers.observe === undefined) {
+    defects.push({ path: child(path, 'observe'), problem: 'required while a template is observe-signal or observe-history; no window is implied' });
+  }
+  // Two walks of one branch would each record the same reversal and race to close the same window.
+  if (schedules.filter((s) => s.template === 'observe-history').length > 1) {
+    defects.push({ path: child(path, 'schedules'), problem: `'observe-history' is named by more than one schedule; one walk keeps the record` });
   }
   schedules.forEach((s, i) => {
+    if (isSignalTemplate(s.template)) {
+      defects.push({ path: `${child(path, 'schedules')}[${String(i)}].template`, problem: `'${s.template}' records a delivery that names a commit; a schedule's firing names none` });
+    }
     if (s.template === 'nightly-security' && triggers.nightly === undefined) {
       defects.push({ path: `${child(path, 'schedules')}[${String(i)}].template`, problem: `'nightly-security' needs a nightly block naming its scanners and advisory host` });
     }
   });
 }

@@ -426,98 +432,100 @@ export function parkRefusal(state: RunState, task: Task, limits: StepLimits, con
 /**
  * The next thing the line does, from committed state alone.
  *
  * `build` builds every task whose dependencies have passed and that has not,
  * up to `limits.maxParallelTasks` at once, in graph order (R4); `verify`
  * verifies one task at a time, every task
  * `build` left verifying; leaving `verify` goes back to `build` while any build
  * task has not passed, and on to `review` once all have. A task whose status is
  * `running` was in flight when the run stopped, and is run again.
  */
 export function nextStep(state: RunState, graph: TaskGraph, limits: StepLimits, contracts: StationContractTable = STATION_CONTRACTS): Step {
   if (!Number.isInteger(limits.maxParallelTasks) || limits.maxParallelTasks < 1) {
     throw new Error(`station machine: ${String(limits.maxParallelTasks)} tasks at once is not a positive integer; refused rather than building none`);
   }
   // A cancelled run does nothing more, whatever else its state holds (A-P9-01).
   if (state.cancelled !== null) {
     const { by, at } = state.cancelled;
     return {
       kind: 'refuse',
       refusal: { ok: false, reason: 'cancelled', cancelledBy: by, cancelledAt: at, message: `the run was cancelled by ${by} at ${at} and does not continue` },
     };
   }
   // A drive that ended in an error stopped the run without saying why in its state; the halt is that record (A-P9-02).
   if (state.halted !== null) {
     const { at, message } = state.halted;
     return { kind: 'refuse', refusal: { ok: false, reason: 'halted', haltedAt: at, message: `a drive of the run ended in an error at ${at} and the run does not continue: ${message}` } };
   }
   if (state.violations.length > 0) {
     return {
       kind: 'refuse',
       refusal: {
         ok: false,
         reason: 'violation',
         violations: [...state.violations],
         message: `the run has ${String(state.violations.length)} recorded integrity violation(s) and does not continue`,
       },
     };
   }
   const parked = graph.tasks.find((task) => statusOf(state, task) === 'parked');
   if (parked !== undefined) return { kind: 'refuse', refusal: parkRefusal(state, parked, limits, contracts) };
   // A drafting seat parks like a task, and a person's rejection of a station's last draft parks the run (R19).
   for (const station of DRAFT_STATIONS) {
     for (const seat of ['drafter', 'critic'] as const) {
       const task = seatTask(state, station, seat);
       if (statusOf(state, task) === 'parked') return { kind: 'refuse', refusal: parkRefusal(state, task, limits, contracts) };
     }
     if (state.drafts[station]?.parked === true) return { kind: 'refuse', refusal: draftsRejected(station, contracts) };
   }
 
   const builds = graph.tasks.filter((task) => task.station === 'build');
   const reviews = graph.tasks.filter((task) => task.station === 'review');
 
+  // A run at `observe` was merged; its window is the history walk's to close, in either phase (D-R8a-04).
+  if (state.station === 'observe') return { kind: 'observe' };
+
   if (state.phase === 'exiting') {
     if (state.station === 'verify') {
       if (builds.every((task) => statusOf(state, task) === 'passed')) return { kind: 'exit', from: 'verify', to: 'review' };
       if (!builds.some((task) => BUILDABLE.has(statusOf(state, task) ?? 'pending') && depsPassed(state, task))) {
         throw new Error('station machine: leaving verify with build tasks unpassed, and none of them can be built; the graph cannot finish');
       }
       return { kind: 'exit', from: 'verify', to: 'build' };
     }
     const to = successor(state.station);
     if (to === null) throw new Error(`station machine: there is no station after ${state.station}`);
     return { kind: 'exit', from: state.station, to };
   }
 
   switch (state.station) {
     case 'build': {
       const [first, ...rest] = builds.filter((t) => BUILDABLE.has(statusOf(state, t) ?? 'pending') && depsPassed(state, t)).slice(0, limits.maxParallelTasks);
       return first === undefined ? { kind: 'finish', station: 'build' } : { kind: 'build', tasks: [first, ...rest] };
     }
     case 'verify': {
       const task = builds.find((t) => statusOf(state, t) === 'verifying');
       return task === undefined ? { kind: 'finish', station: 'verify' } : { kind: 'verify', task };
     }
     case 'review': {
       const task = reviews.find((t) => {
         const status = statusOf(state, t) ?? 'pending';
         return status === 'pending' || status === 'running';
       });
       return task === undefined ? { kind: 'finish', station: 'review' } : { kind: 'review', task };
     }
     case 'intake':
     case 'spec':
     case 'test-design':
     case 'plan':
     case 'integrate':
       return { kind: 'work', station: state.station };
-    case 'observe':
     case 'learn':
-      throw new Error(`station machine: M1 never enters ${state.station}`);
+      throw new Error('station machine: M3 enters learn, and the line never does');
   }
 }
